===== M0004 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParameters(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 867-892
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
867 |   private void updateTypeOfParameters(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
868 |     int i = 0;
869 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
870 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
871 |       if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
872 |         // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
873 |         return;
874 |       }
875 | 
876 |       JSType iParameterType = iParameter.getJSType();
877 |       Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
878 |       JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
879 |       inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(iArgumentType, iParameterType);
880 | 
881 |       if (iParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
882 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = (FunctionType) iParameterType;
883 | 
884 |         if (iArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
885 |             iArgumentType instanceof FunctionType &&
886 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
887 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
888 |         }
889 |       }
890 |       i++;
891 |     }
892 |   }

===== M0006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1758-1775
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
1758 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
1759 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1760 |     if (info == null) {
1761 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
1762 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
1763 |       if (parentType == Token.NAME) {
1764 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1765 |         if (info == null && parent.getParent().hasOneChild()) {
1766 |           info = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
1767 |         }
1768 |       } else if (parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
1769 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1770 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
1771 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
1772 |       }
1773 |     }
1774 |     return info;
1775 |   }

===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 441-456
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
441 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
442 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
443 |       Set<String> directives = null;
444 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
445 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
446 |         if (directives == null) {
447 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
448 |         } else {
449 |           directives.add(directive);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 | 
453 |       if (directives != null) {
454 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
455 |       }
456 |     }

/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
558 |       return scope;
559 |     }
560 | 
561 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
562 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
563 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
564 |       scopes.push(scope);
565 |     }
566 |     scopeRoots.clear();
567 | 
568 |     return scope;
569 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 129-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
129 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
130 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
131 |     String methodName = null;
132 |     if (callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
133 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
134 |     } else if (callName.getType() == Token.NAME) {
135 |       String name = callName.getString();
136 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
137 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
138 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
139 |       }
140 |     }
141 | 
142 |     if (methodName != null) {
143 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
144 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
145 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
146 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
147 |       }
148 |     }
149 |     return null;
150 |   }

===== M0027 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0032 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionType.resolveInternal(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionType.java
Original range: 510-528
Documentation:

Method code:
510 |   @Override
511 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
512 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this); // for circularly defined types.
513 | 
514 |     boolean changed = false;
515 |     ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> resolvedTypes = ImmutableList.builder();
516 |     for (JSType alternate : alternates) {
517 |       JSType newAlternate = alternate.resolve(t, scope);
518 |       changed |= (alternate != newAlternate);
519 |       resolvedTypes.add(alternate);
520 |     }
521 |     if (changed) {
522 |       Collection<JSType> newAlternates = resolvedTypes.build();

===== M0035 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 443-489
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
445 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
446 | 
447 |       switch (n.getType()) {
448 |         case Token.CALL:
449 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
450 |           break;
451 | 
452 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
453 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
454 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
455 |           }
456 | 
457 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
458 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
459 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);
460 |           }
461 |           break;
462 | 
463 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
464 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
465 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
466 |           if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
467 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
468 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
469 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
470 |           }
471 |           break;
472 | 
473 |         case Token.CATCH:
474 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
475 |           break;
476 | 
477 |         case Token.VAR:
478 |           defineVar(n, parent);
479 |           break;
480 | 
481 |         case Token.GETPROP:
482 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
483 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
484 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
485 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
486 |           }
487 |           break;
488 |       }
489 |     }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseHook(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 763-795
Documentation:

Method code:
763 |   private FlowScope traverseHook(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
764 |     Node condition = n.getFirstChild();
765 |     Node trueNode = condition.getNext();
766 |     Node falseNode = n.getLastChild();
767 | 
768 |     // verify the condition
769 |     scope = traverse(condition, scope);
770 | 
771 |     // reverse abstract interpret the condition to produce two new scopes
772 |     FlowScope trueScope = reverseInterpreter.
773 |         getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
774 |             condition, scope, true);
775 |     FlowScope falseScope = reverseInterpreter.
776 |         getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
777 |             condition, scope, false);
778 | 
779 |     // traverse the true node with the trueScope
780 |     traverse(trueNode, trueScope.createChildFlowScope());
781 | 
782 |     // traverse the false node with the falseScope
783 |     traverse(falseNode, falseScope.createChildFlowScope());
784 | 
785 |     // meet true and false nodes' types and assign
786 |     JSType trueType = trueNode.getJSType();
787 |     JSType falseType = falseNode.getJSType();
788 |     if (trueType != null && falseType != null) {
789 |       n.setJSType(trueType.getLeastSupertype(falseType));
790 |     } else {
791 |       n.setJSType(null);
792 |     }
793 | 
794 |     return scope.createChildFlowScope();
795 |   }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.diffSlots(StaticSlot<JSType>,StaticSlot<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 308-320
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether two slots are meaningfully different for the
   * purposes of data flow analysis.
   */
Method code:
308 |   private boolean diffSlots(StaticSlot<JSType> slotA,
309 |                             StaticSlot<JSType> slotB) {
310 |     boolean aIsNull = slotA == null || slotA.getType() == null;
311 |     boolean bIsNull = slotB == null || slotB.getType() == null;
312 |     if (aIsNull && bIsNull) {
313 |       return false;
314 |     } else if (aIsNull ^ bIsNull) {
315 |       return true;
316 |     }
317 | 
318 |     // Both slots and types must be non-null.
319 |     return slotA.getType().differsFrom(slotB.getType());
320 |   }

===== M0049 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 226-282
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
226 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
227 |     this.options = options;
228 |     if (errorManager == null) {
229 |       if (outStream == null) {
230 |         setErrorManager(
231 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
232 |       } else {
233 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
234 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
235 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
236 |         setErrorManager(printer);
237 |       }
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
241 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
242 |       options.checkTypes = true;
243 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = false;
245 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
246 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
247 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
248 |       // parser type warnings.
249 |       options.setWarningLevel(
250 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
251 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
252 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
253 |     }
254 | 
255 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
256 |       options.setWarningLevel(
257 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
258 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
259 |     }
260 | 
261 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
262 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
263 |     guards.add(
264 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
265 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
266 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
267 | 
268 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
269 | 
270 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
271 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
272 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
273 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
274 |     // error off.
275 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
276 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
277 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
282 |   }

===== M0053 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 166-206
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
166 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
167 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
168 |     switch (n.getType()) {
169 |       case Token.STRING:
170 |         return n.getString();
171 | 
172 |       case Token.NAME:
173 |         String name = n.getString();
174 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
175 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
176 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
177 |           return name;
178 |         }
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.NUMBER:
182 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
183 | 
184 |       case Token.FALSE:
185 |       case Token.TRUE:
186 |       case Token.NULL:
187 |         return Node.tokenToName(n.getType());
188 | 
189 |       case Token.VOID:
190 |         return "undefined";
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NOT:
193 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
194 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
195 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
196 |         }
197 |         break;
198 | 
199 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
200 |         return arrayToString(n);
201 | 
202 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
203 |         return "[object Object]";
204 |     }
205 |     return null;
206 |   }

/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1752 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1753 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1754 |       return getString();
1755 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1756 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1757 |       if (left == null) {
1758 |         return null;
1759 |       }
1760 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1761 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1762 |       return "this";
1763 |     } else {
1764 |       return null;
1765 |     }
1766 |   }

     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).

1162 |               (FunctionType) rValue.getJSType(), info, lValue)) {
1163 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1164 |       } else if (info != null) {
1165 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1166 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1167 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1168 |           } else {
1169 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1170 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1171 |           }
1172 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1173 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1174 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1175 |         } else {
1176 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1177 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1178 |             JSType knownType = null;
1179 |             if (rValue != null) {
1180 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1181 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1182 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1183 |               } else if (rValue.getType() == Token.OR) {
1184 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1185 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1186 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1187 |                 // reasons.
1188 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1189 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1190 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.getType() == Token.NAME
1191 |                     && lValue.getType() == Token.NAME
1192 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1193 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1194 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1195 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1196 |                 }
1197 |               }
1198 |             }
1199 |           }
1200 |         }
1201 |       }
1202 | 
1203 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1204 |     }

===== M0080 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 899-989
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
899 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
900 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
901 | 
902 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
903 |       return;
904 |     }
905 | 
906 |     int i = 0;
907 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
908 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
909 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
910 |       JSType iParameterType =
911 |           getJSType(iParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
912 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
913 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
914 |         JSType iArgumentType = null;
915 |         if (i + 1 < childCount) {
916 |           Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
917 |           iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
918 |           if (!(iArgumentType instanceof ObjectType)) {
919 |             compiler.report(
920 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
921 |                     TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE));
922 |             return;
923 |           }
924 |         }
925 | 
926 |         // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
927 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
928 |         int j = 0;
929 |         for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
930 |           JSType jParameterType =
931 |               getJSType(jParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
932 |           if (jParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
933 |             FunctionType jParameterFnType = (FunctionType) jParameterType;
934 |             if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
935 |               foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
936 |               // Find the actual type of the this argument.
937 |               if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
938 |                 // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
939 |                 return;
940 |               }
941 |               Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
942 |               JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
943 |               if (jArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
944 |                   jArgumentType instanceof FunctionType) {
945 |                 if (iArgumentType != null &&
946 |                     // null and undefined get filtered out above.
947 |                     !iArgumentType.isNoType()) {
948 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
949 |                   // using the actual type of T.
950 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType = (FunctionType) jArgumentType;
951 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
952 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
953 |                     // function.
954 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
955 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
956 |                             jArgumentFnType, (ObjectType) iArgumentType));
957 |                   }
958 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal does not
959 |                   // reference this.
960 |                   if (!NodeUtil.referencesThis(
961 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
962 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
963 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNREAD_THIS));
964 |                   }
965 |                 } else {
966 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal references this.
967 |                   if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(
968 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
969 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
970 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNDEFINED_THIS));
971 |                   }
972 |                 }
973 |               }
974 |               // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
975 |               // types of the arguments match.
976 |             }
977 |           }
978 |           j++;
979 |         }
980 | 
981 |         if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
982 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
983 |               TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
984 |           return;
985 |         }
986 |       }
987 |       i++;
988 |     }
989 |   }

===== M0086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1064-1084
Documentation:
/**
   * Suppose X is an object with inferred properties.
   * Suppose also that X is used in a way where it would only type-check
   * correctly if some of those properties are widened.
   * Then we should be polite and automatically widen X's properties for him.
   *
   * For a concrete example, consider:
   * param x {{prop: (number|undefined)}}
   * function f(x) {}
   * f({});
   *
   * If we give the anonymous object an inferred property of (number|undefined),
   * then this code will type-check appropriately.
   */
Method code:
1064 |   private void inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(
1065 |       JSType type, JSType constraint) {
1066 |     ObjectType constraintObj =
1067 |         ObjectType.cast(constraint.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1068 |     if (constraintObj != null && constraintObj.isRecordType()) {
1069 |       ObjectType objType = ObjectType.cast(type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1070 |       if (objType != null) {
1071 |         for (String prop : constraintObj.getOwnPropertyNames()) {
1072 |           JSType propType = constraintObj.getPropertyType(prop);
1073 |           if (!objType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(prop)) {
1074 |             JSType typeToInfer = propType;
1075 |             if (!objType.hasProperty(prop)) {
1076 |               typeToInfer =
1077 |                   getNativeType(VOID_TYPE).getLeastSupertype(propType);
1078 |             }
1079 |             objType.defineInferredProperty(prop, typeToInfer, false, null);
1080 |           }
1081 |         }
1082 |       }
1083 |     }
1084 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 118-282
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {
271 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getTypesUnderInequality(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 768-788
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the subset of {@code this} and {@code that} types if inequality
   * is observed. If a value {@code v1} of type {@code number} is not equal to a
   * value {@code v2} of type {@code (undefined,number)}, we can infer that the
   * type of {@code v1} is {@code number} and the type of {@code v2} is
   * {@code number} as well.
   *
   * @return a pair containing the restricted type of {@code this} as the first
   *         component and the restricted type of {@code that} as the second
   *         element. The returned pair is never {@code null} even though its
   *         components may be {@code null}
   */
Method code:
768 |   public TypePair getTypesUnderInequality(JSType that) {
769 |     // unions types
770 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
771 |       TypePair p = that.getTypesUnderInequality(this);
772 |       return new TypePair(p.typeB, p.typeA);
773 |     }
774 | 
775 |     // other types
776 |     switch (this.testForEquality(that)) {
777 |       case TRUE:
778 |         JSType noType = getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE);
779 |         return new TypePair(noType, noType);
780 | 
781 |       case FALSE:
782 |       case UNKNOWN:
783 |         return new TypePair(this, that);
784 |     }
785 | 
786 |     // switch case is exhaustive
787 |     throw new IllegalStateException();
788 |   }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1658-1677
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the condition of an ON_TRUE / ON_FALSE CFG edge.
   * @param n a node with an outgoing conditional CFG edge
   * @return the condition node or null if the condition is not obviously a node
   */
Method code:
1658 |   static Node getConditionExpression(Node n) {
1659 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1660 |       case Token.IF:
1661 |       case Token.WHILE:
1662 |         return n.getFirstChild();
1663 |       case Token.DO:
1664 |         return n.getLastChild();
1665 |       case Token.FOR:
1666 |         switch (n.getChildCount()) {
1667 |           case 3:
1668 |             return null;
1669 |           case 4:
1670 |             return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
1671 |         }
1672 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("malformed 'for' statement " + n);
1673 |       case Token.CASE:
1674 |         return null;
1675 |     }
1676 |     throw new IllegalArgumentException(n + " does not have a condition.");
1677 |   }

===== M0104 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

===== M0107 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 313-343
Documentation:
/**
   * If we haven't found a return value yet, try to look at the "return"
   * statements in the function.
   */
Method code:
313 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(
314 |       @Nullable Node functionBlock) {
315 |     if (functionBlock == null || compiler.getInput(sourceName).isExtern()) {
316 |       return this;
317 |     }
318 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(functionBlock.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
319 |     if (returnType == null) {
320 |       boolean hasNonEmptyReturns = false;
321 |       List<Node> worklist = Lists.newArrayList(functionBlock);
322 |       while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
323 |         Node current = worklist.remove(worklist.size() - 1);
324 |         int cType = current.getType();
325 |         if (cType == Token.RETURN && current.getFirstChild() != null) {
326 |           hasNonEmptyReturns = true;
327 |           break;
328 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(current) ||
329 |             NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
330 |           for (Node child = current.getFirstChild();
331 |                child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
332 |             worklist.add(child);
333 |           }
334 |         }
335 |       }
336 | 
337 |       if (!hasNonEmptyReturns) {
338 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
339 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
340 |       }
341 |     }
342 |     return this;
343 |   }

/**
     * Handle typedefs.

Method code:
1606 |     private void checkForTypedef(
1607 |         NodeTraversal t, Node candidate, JSDocInfo info) {
1608 |       if (info == null || !info.hasTypedefType()) {
1609 |         return;
1610 |       }
1611 |

1613 |       if (typedef == null) {
1614 |         return;
1615 |       }
1616 | 
1617 |       // TODO(nicksantos|user): This is a terrible, terrible hack
1618 |       // to bail out on recusive typedefs. We'll eventually need
1619 |       // to handle these properly.
1620 |       typeRegistry.declareType(typedef, getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1621 |

1626 |                 t.getSourceName(), candidate, MALFORMED_TYPEDEF, typedef));
1627 |       }
1628 |

1630 |       if (candidate.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
1631 |         defineSlot(candidate, candidate.getParent(),
1632 |             getNativeType(NO_TYPE), false);
1633 |       }
1634 |     }

===== M0109 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 423-449
Documentation:

Method code:
423 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
424 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
425 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
426 |     switch (n.getType()) {
427 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
428 |         // normal type checking
429 |         final TypeCheck outerThis = this;
430 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
431 |         final FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
432 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
433 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
434 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
435 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
436 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
437 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
438 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
439 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
440 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
441 |         }
442 | 
443 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
444 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
445 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
446 |         break;
447 |     }
448 |     return true;
449 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 310-317
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }

===== M0126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.supAndInfHelper(JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 547-623
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the supremum or infimum of functions with other types.
   * Because sup() and inf() share a lot of logic for functions, we use
   * a single helper.
   * @param leastSuper If true, compute the supremum of {@code this} with
   *     {@code that}. Otherwise compute the infimum.
   * @return The least supertype or greatest subtype.
   */
Method code:
547 |   private JSType supAndInfHelper(JSType that, boolean leastSuper) {
548 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): When we remove the unknown type, the function types
549 |     // form a lattice with the universal constructor at the top of the lattice,
550 |     // and the LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE type at the bottom of the lattice.
551 |     //
552 |     // When we introduce the unknown type, it's much more difficult to make
553 |     // heads or tails of the partial ordering of types, because there's no
554 |     // clear hierarchy between the different components (parameter types and
555 |     // return types) in the ArrowType.
556 |     //
557 |     // Rather than make the situation more complicated by introducing new
558 |     // types (like unions of functions), we just fallback on the simpler
559 |     // approach of getting things right at the top and the bottom of the
560 |     // lattice.
561 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
562 |       if (isEquivalentTo(that)) {
563 |         return this;
564 |       }
565 | 
566 |       FunctionType other = null;
567 |       if (that instanceof FunctionType) {
568 |         other = (FunctionType) that;
569 |       }
570 | 
571 |       // If these are ordinary functions, then merge them.
572 |       // Don't do this if any of the params/return
573 |       // values are unknown, because then there will be cycles in
574 |       // their local lattice and they will merge in weird ways.
575 |       if (other != null &&
576 |           isOrdinaryFunction() && that.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
577 |           !this.call.hasUnknownParamsOrReturn() &&
578 |           !other.call.hasUnknownParamsOrReturn()) {
579 | 
580 |         // Check for the degenerate case, but double check
581 |         // that there's not a cycle.
582 |         boolean isSubtypeOfThat = this.isSubtype(that);
583 |         boolean isSubtypeOfThis = that.isSubtype(this);
584 |         if (isSubtypeOfThat && !isSubtypeOfThis) {
585 |           return leastSuper ? that : this;
586 |         } else if (isSubtypeOfThis && !isSubtypeOfThat) {
587 |           return leastSuper ? this : that;
588 |         }
589 | 
590 |         // Merge the two functions component-wise.
591 |         FunctionType merged = tryMergeFunctionPiecewise(other, leastSuper);
592 |         if (merged != null) {
593 |           return merged;
594 |         }
595 |       }
596 | 
597 |       // The function instance type is a special case
598 |       // that lives above the rest of the lattice.
599 |       JSType functionInstance = registry.getNativeType(
600 |           JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE);
601 |       if (functionInstance.isEquivalentTo(that)) {
602 |         return leastSuper ? that : this;
603 |       } else if (functionInstance.isEquivalentTo(this)) {
604 |         return leastSuper ? this : that;
605 |       }
606 | 
607 |       // In theory, we should be using the GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE as the
608 |       // greatest function. In practice, we don't because it's way too
609 |       // broad. The greatest function takes var_args None parameters, which
610 |       // means that all parameters register a type warning.
611 |       //
612 |       // Instead, we use the U2U ctor type, which has unknown type args.
613 |       FunctionType greatestFn =
614 |           registry.getNativeFunctionType(JSTypeNative.U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
615 |       FunctionType leastFn =
616 |           registry.getNativeFunctionType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
617 |       return leastSuper ? greatestFn : leastFn;
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     return leastSuper ?
621 |         super.getLeastSupertype(that) :
622 |         super.getGreatestSubtype(that);
623 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.

===== M0151 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0154 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 494-540
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
494 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
495 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
496 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
497 |     switch (left.getType()) {
498 |       case Token.NAME:
499 |         String varName = left.getString();
500 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
501 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
502 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
503 |         }
504 | 
505 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
506 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
507 |         // This has two purposes:
508 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
509 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
510 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
511 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
512 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
513 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
514 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
515 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
516 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
517 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
518 |         }
519 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
520 |             resultType : null);
521 | 
522 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
523 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
524 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
525 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
526 |         }
527 |         break;
528 |       case Token.GETPROP:
529 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
530 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
531 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
532 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
533 |               resultType);
534 |         }
535 | 
536 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
537 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
538 |         break;
539 |     }
540 |   }

988 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Handle this correctly if we have a UnionType.
989 |     //
990 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the runtime coerces "null" to the global
991 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
992 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the
993 |     // fact that when most people write @this {Foo}, they really don't
994 |     // mean "nullable Foo". For certain tags (like @extends) we de-nullify
995 |     // the name for them.
996 |     JSType maybeTypeOfThis = safeResolve(typeOfThis, t, scope);
997 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis != null) {
998 |       maybeTypeOfThis = maybeTypeOfThis.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
999 |     }
1000 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis instanceof ObjectType) {
1001 |       typeOfThis = (ObjectType) maybeTypeOfThis;
1002 |     }
1003 | 
1004 |     boolean changed = false;
1005 |     ImmutableList.Builder<ObjectType> resolvedInterfaces =
1006 |         ImmutableList.builder();
1007 |     for (ObjectType iface : implementedInterfaces) {
1008 |       ObjectType resolvedIface = (ObjectType) iface.resolve(t, scope);
1009 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
1010 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
1011 |     }
1012 |     if (changed) {
1013 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
1014 |     }
1015 | 
1016 |     if (subTypes != null) {
1017 |       for (int i = 0; i < subTypes.size(); i++) {
1018 |         subTypes.set(i, (FunctionType) subTypes.get(i).resolve(t, scope));
1019 |       }
1020 |     }
1021 | 
1022 |     return super.resolveInternal(t, scope);
1023 |   }

===== M0161 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.allFlowSlots()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 340-356
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets all the symbols that have been defined before this point
   * in the current flow. Does not return slots that have not changed during
   * the flow.
   *
   * For example, consider the code:
   * <code>
   * var x = 3;
   * function f() {
   *   var y = 5;
   *   y = 6; // FLOW POINT
   *   var z = y;
   *   return z;
   * }
   * </code>
   * A FlowScope at FLOW POINT will return a slot for y, but not
   * a slot for x or z.
   */
Method code:
340 |   private Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> allFlowSlots() {
341 |     Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slots = Maps.newHashMap();
342 |     for (LinkedFlowSlot slot = lastSlot;
343 |          slot != null; slot = slot.parent) {
344 |       if (!slots.containsKey(slot.getName())) {
345 |         slots.put(slot.getName(), slot);
346 |       }
347 |     }
348 | 
349 |     for (String key : cache.symbols.keySet()) {
350 |       if (!slots.containsKey(key)) {
351 |         slots.put(key, cache.symbols.get(key));
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     return slots;
356 |   }

===== M0169 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1685-1710
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1685 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1686 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1687 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1688 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1689 |       skipEOLs();
1690 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1691 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1692 |       skipEOLs();
1693 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1694 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1695 |       skipEOLs();
1696 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1697 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1698 |       String string = stream.getString();
1699 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1700 |         skipEOLs();
1701 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1702 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1703 |         return newStringNode(string);
1704 |       } else {
1705 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1706 |       }
1707 |     }
1708 | 
1709 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1710 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 304-336
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
304 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
305 |     if (prototype == null) {
306 |       return false;
307 |     }
308 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
309 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
310 |       return false;
311 |     }
312 | 
313 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
314 |     this.prototype = prototype;
315 | 
316 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
317 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
318 |       if (superClass != null) {
319 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       if (isInterface()) {
323 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
324 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
325 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
326 |           }
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 |     }
330 | 
331 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
332 |       clearCachedValues();
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     return true;
336 |   }

===== M0186 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

===== M0194 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseFunctionType(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1757-1826
Documentation:
/**
   * FunctionType := 'function' FunctionSignatureType
   * FunctionSignatureType :=
   *    TypeParameters '(' 'this' ':' TypeName, ParametersType ')' ResultType
   */
Method code:
1757 |   private Node parseFunctionType(JsDocToken token) {
1758 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): We're not implementing generics at the moment, so
1759 |     // just throw out TypeParameters.
1760 |     if (token != JsDocToken.LP) {
1761 |       return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.lp");
1762 |     }
1763 | 
1764 |     Node functionType = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
1765 |     Node parameters = null;
1766 |     skipEOLs();
1767 |     if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
1768 |       token = next();
1769 | 
1770 |       boolean hasParams = true;
1771 |       if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1772 |         String tokenStr = stream.getString();
1773 |         boolean isThis = "this".equals(tokenStr);
1774 |         boolean isNew = "new".equals(tokenStr);
1775 |         if (isThis || isNew) {
1776 |           if (match(JsDocToken.COLON)) {
1777 |             next();
1778 |             skipEOLs();
1779 |             Node contextType = wrapNode(
1780 |                 isThis ? Token.THIS : Token.NEW,
1781 |                 parseTypeName(next()));
1782 |             if (contextType == null) {
1783 |               return null;
1784 |             }
1785 | 
1786 |             functionType.addChildToFront(contextType);
1787 |           } else {
1788 |             return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.colon");
1789 |           }
1790 | 
1791 |           if (match(JsDocToken.COMMA)) {
1792 |             next();
1793 |             skipEOLs();
1794 |             token = next();
1795 |           } else {
1796 |             hasParams = false;
1797 |           }
1798 |         }
1799 |       }
1800 | 
1801 |       if (hasParams) {
1802 |         parameters = parseParametersType(token);
1803 |         if (parameters == null) {
1804 |           return null;
1805 |         }
1806 |       }
1807 |     }
1808 | 
1809 |     if (parameters != null) {
1810 |       functionType.addChildToBack(parameters);
1811 |     }
1812 | 
1813 |     skipEOLs();
1814 |     if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
1815 |       return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rp");
1816 |     }
1817 | 
1818 |     skipEOLs();
1819 |     Node resultType = parseResultType(next());
1820 |     if (resultType == null) {
1821 |       return null;
1822 |     } else {
1823 |       functionType.addChildToBack(resultType);
1824 |     }
1825 |     return functionType;
1826 |   }

===== M0204 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1473-1535
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1473 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1474 |     FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
1475 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1476 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1477 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.
1478 |           getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype().getConstructor();
1479 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1480 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1481 |           (baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1482 |         compiler.report(
1483 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1484 |       } else {
1485 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1486 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1487 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1488 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1489 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1490 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1491 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1492 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1493 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1494 |               badImplementedType = true;
1495 |             }
1496 |           } else {
1497 |             badImplementedType = true;
1498 |           }
1499 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1500 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1501 |           }
1502 |         }
1503 |         // check properties
1504 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1505 |       }
1506 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1507 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1508 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1509 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1510 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1511 |           compiler.report(
1512 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1513 |         }
1514 |       }
1515 |       // Interface cannot implement any interfaces
1516 |       if (functionType.hasImplementedInterfaces()) {
1517 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
1518 |             CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1519 |       }
1520 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1521 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1522 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1523 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1524 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1525 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1526 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1527 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1528 |           currentProperties.clear();
1529 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1530 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1531 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1532 |         }
1533 |       }
1534 |     }
1535 |   }

===== M0206 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.FlatFlowScopeCache.FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope,LinkedFlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 412-476
Documentation:
// A cache at the join of two scope chains.
Method code:
412 |     FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeA,
413 |         LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeB) {
414 |       linkedEquivalent = null;
415 | 
416 |       // Always prefer the "real" function scope to the faked-out
417 |       // bottom scope.
418 |       functionScope = joinedScopeA.flowsFromBottom() ?
419 |           joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope() : joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope();
420 | 
421 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsA = joinedScopeA.allFlowSlots();
422 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsB = joinedScopeB.allFlowSlots();
423 | 
424 |       symbols = slotsA;
425 | 
426 |       // There are 5 different join cases:
427 |       // 1) The type is declared in joinedScopeA, not in joinedScopeB,
428 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in A.
429 |       // 2) The type is declared in joinedScopeB, not in joinedScopeA,
430 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in B.
431 |       // 3) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeA, but
432 |       //    not in joinedScopeB. Join the two types.
433 |       // 4) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeB, but
434 |       //    not in joinedScopeA. Join the two types.
435 |       // 5) The type is declared in joinedScopeA and joinedScopeB. Join
436 |       //    the two types.
437 |       Set<String> symbolNames = Sets.newHashSet(symbols.keySet());
438 |       symbolNames.addAll(slotsB.keySet());
439 | 
440 |       for (String name : symbolNames) {
441 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotA = slotsA.get(name);
442 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotB = slotsB.get(name);
443 | 
444 |         JSType joinedType = null;
445 |         if (slotB == null || slotB.getType() == null) {
446 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
447 |               = joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
448 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
449 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
450 |             // Case #1 -- already inserted.
451 |           } else {
452 |             // Case #3
453 |             joinedType = slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
454 |           }
455 |         } else if (slotA == null || slotA.getType() == null) {
456 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
457 |               = joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
458 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
459 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
460 |             // Case #2
461 |             symbols.put(name, slotB);
462 |           } else {
463 |             // Case #4
464 |             joinedType = slotB.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
465 |           }
466 |         } else {
467 |           // Case #5
468 |           joinedType =
469 |               slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(slotB.getType());
470 |         }
471 | 
472 |         if (joinedType != null) {
473 |           symbols.put(name, new SimpleSlot(name, joinedType, true));
474 |         }
475 |       }
476 |     }

1102 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1103 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1104 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1105 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1106 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1107 |     if (var != null) {
1108 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1109 |       if (varType != null) {
1110 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1111 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1112 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1113 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1114 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1115 |         } else {
1116 |           return varType;
1117 |         }
1118 |       }
1119 |     }

1122 |     if (objType != null) {
1123 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1124 |     }

1130 |       if (regType != null) {

1132 |       }
1133 |     }
1134 |

1136 |   }
