===== M0212 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 461-812
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
461 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
462 |     JSType childType;
463 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
464 |     Node left, right;
465 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
466 |     boolean typeable = true;
467 | 
468 |     switch (n.getType()) {
469 |       case Token.NAME:
470 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.LP:
474 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
475 |         // ignored here.
476 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
477 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
478 |         } else {
479 |           typeable = false;
480 |         }
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.COMMA:
484 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
485 |         break;
486 | 
487 |       case Token.TRUE:
488 |       case Token.FALSE:
489 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.THIS:
493 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
494 |         break;
495 | 
496 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
497 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.GET_REF:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.NULL:
505 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
506 |         break;
507 | 
508 |       case Token.NUMBER:
509 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
510 |         break;
511 | 
512 |       case Token.STRING:
513 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
514 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
515 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
516 |         }
517 |         break;
518 | 
519 |       case Token.GET:
520 |       case Token.SET:
521 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
522 |         break;
523 | 
524 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
525 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
526 |         break;
527 | 
528 |       case Token.REGEXP:
529 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
530 |         break;
531 | 
532 |       case Token.GETPROP:
533 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
534 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
535 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
536 |         break;
537 | 
538 |       case Token.GETELEM:
539 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
540 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
541 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
542 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
543 |         typeable = false;
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.VAR:
547 |         visitVar(t, n);
548 |         typeable = false;
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NEW:
552 |         visitNew(t, n);
553 |         typeable = true;
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.CALL:
557 |         visitCall(t, n);
558 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.RETURN:
562 |         visitReturn(t, n);
563 |         typeable = false;
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.DEC:
567 |       case Token.INC:
568 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
569 |         validator.expectNumber(
570 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
571 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
572 |         break;
573 | 
574 |       case Token.NOT:
575 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
576 |         break;
577 | 
578 |       case Token.VOID:
579 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
583 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
584 |         break;
585 | 
586 |       case Token.BITNOT:
587 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
588 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
589 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
590 |               childType.toString());
591 |         }
592 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
593 |         break;
594 | 
595 |       case Token.POS:
596 |       case Token.NEG:
597 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
598 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
599 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
600 |         break;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.EQ:
603 |       case Token.NE: {
604 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
605 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
606 | 
607 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
608 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
609 |         TernaryValue result =
610 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
611 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
612 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
613 |             result = result.not();
614 |           }
615 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
616 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
617 |         }
618 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
619 |         break;
620 |       }
621 | 
622 |       case Token.SHEQ:
623 |       case Token.SHNE: {
624 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
625 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
626 | 
627 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
628 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
629 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
630 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
631 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
632 |               rightType.toString());
633 |         }
634 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
635 |         break;
636 |       }
637 | 
638 |       case Token.LT:
639 |       case Token.LE:
640 |       case Token.GT:
641 |       case Token.GE:
642 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
643 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
644 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
645 |           validator.expectNumber(
646 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
647 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
648 |           validator.expectNumber(
649 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
650 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
651 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
652 |           // OK.
653 |         } else {
654 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
655 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
656 |           // should match a string context.
657 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
658 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
659 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
660 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
661 |           message = "right side of comparison";
662 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
663 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
664 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
665 |         }
666 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
667 |         break;
668 | 
669 |       case Token.IN:
670 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
671 |         right = n.getLastChild();
672 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
673 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
674 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
675 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
676 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
677 |         break;
678 | 
679 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
680 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
681 |         right = n.getLastChild();
682 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
683 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
684 | 
685 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
686 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
687 |         validator.expectActualObject(
688 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
689 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
690 |         break;
691 | 
692 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
693 |         visitAssign(t, n);
694 |         typeable = false;
695 |         break;
696 | 
697 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
698 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
699 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
702 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
703 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
704 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
705 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
706 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
707 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
708 |       case Token.LSH:
709 |       case Token.RSH:
710 |       case Token.URSH:
711 |       case Token.DIV:
712 |       case Token.MOD:
713 |       case Token.BITOR:
714 |       case Token.BITXOR:
715 |       case Token.BITAND:
716 |       case Token.SUB:
717 |       case Token.ADD:
718 |       case Token.MUL:
719 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
720 |         break;
721 | 
722 |       case Token.DELPROP:
723 |         if (!isReference(n.getFirstChild())) {
724 |           report(t, n, BAD_DELETE);
725 |         }
726 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
727 |         break;
728 | 
729 |       case Token.CASE:
730 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
731 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
732 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
733 |         typeable = false;
734 |         break;
735 | 
736 |       case Token.WITH: {
737 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
738 |         childType = getJSType(child);
739 |         validator.expectObject(
740 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
741 |         typeable = false;
742 |         break;
743 |       }
744 | 
745 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
746 |         visitFunction(t, n);
747 |         break;
748 | 
749 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
750 |       case Token.LABEL:
751 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
752 |       case Token.SWITCH:
753 |       case Token.BREAK:
754 |       case Token.CATCH:
755 |       case Token.TRY:
756 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
757 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
758 |       case Token.BLOCK:
759 |       case Token.EMPTY:
760 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
761 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
762 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
763 |       case Token.THROW:
764 |         typeable = false;
765 |         break;
766 | 
767 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
768 |       case Token.DO:
769 |       case Token.FOR:
770 |       case Token.IF:
771 |       case Token.WHILE:
772 |         typeable = false;
773 |         break;
774 | 
775 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
776 |       case Token.AND:
777 |       case Token.HOOK:
778 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
779 |       case Token.OR:
780 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
781 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
782 |         } else {
783 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
784 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)
785 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
786 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
787 |           } else {
788 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
789 |           }
790 |         }
791 |         if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
792 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
793 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
794 |           }
795 |         }
796 |         break;
797 | 
798 |       default:
799 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
800 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
801 |         break;
802 |     }
803 | 
804 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
805 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
806 | 
807 |     if (typeable) {
808 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
809 |     }
810 | 
811 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
812 |   }

/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1258 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1259 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1260 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1261 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1262 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1263 |       return;
1264 |     }
1265 | 
1266 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1267 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1268 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1269 |     // and what isn't.
1270 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1271 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1272 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1273 | 
1274 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1275 |     // non-object.
1276 |     if (!validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(t, n, childType,
1277 |             childType + " has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1278 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1279 |       return;
1280 |     }
1281 | 
1282 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1283 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1284 |   }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
169 |   boolean parse() {
170 |     int lineno;
171 |     int charno;
172 | 
173 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
174 |     JSTypeExpression type;
175 | 
176 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
177 |     skipEOLs();
178 | 
179 |     JsDocToken token = next();
180 | 
181 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
182 | 
183 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
184 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
185 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
186 |       token = blockInfo.token;
187 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
188 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
189 |       }
190 |     } else {
191 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
192 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
193 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
194 |         // what it was.
195 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
196 |       }
197 |     }
198 | 
199 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
200 |     retry: for (;;) {
201 |       switch (token) {
202 |         case ANNOTATION:
203 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
204 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
205 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
206 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
207 | 
208 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
209 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
210 |             if (annotation == null) {
211 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
212 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
213 |             } else {
214 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
215 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
216 | 
217 |               switch (annotation) {
218 |                 case AUTHOR:
219 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
220 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
221 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
222 | 
223 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
224 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
225 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |                     } else {
227 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
228 |                     }
229 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
230 |                   } else {
231 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
232 |                   }
233 |                   continue retry;
234 | 
235 |                 case CONSTANT:
236 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
237 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
238 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                   }
240 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
241 |                   continue retry;
242 | 
243 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
244 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
245 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
246 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
247 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
248 |                     } else {
249 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
250 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                     }
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case DEPRECATED:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
258 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
259 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
260 |                   }

263 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
264 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
265 |

267 |

270 |                   }
271 | 
272 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
273 |                   continue retry;
274 | 
275 |                 case INTERFACE:
276 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
277 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
278 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
279 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                     } else {
281 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
282 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
283 |                     }
284 |                   }
285 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
286 |                   continue retry;
287 | 
288 |                 case DESC:
289 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
290 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
291 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
292 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
293 |                     continue retry;
294 |                   } else {
295 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
296 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
297 |

485 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
486 |                   }
487 |                   continue retry;
488 | 
489 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
490 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
491 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
492 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
493 |                   }
494 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
495 |                   continue retry;
496 | 
497 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
498 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
499 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
500 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
501 |                   }
502 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
503 |                   continue retry;
504 | 
505 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
506 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
507 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
508 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
509 |                   }
510 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
511 |                   continue retry;
512 | 
513 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
514 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
515 |                   continue retry;
516 | 
517 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
518 |                 case OVERRIDE:
519 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
520 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
521 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
522 |                   }
523 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
524 |                   continue retry;
525 | 
526 |                 case THROWS:
527 |                   skipEOLs();
528 |                   token = next();
529 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
530 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
531 |                   type = null;
532 | 
533 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
534 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
535 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
536 | 
537 |                     if (type == null) {
538 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
539 |                       // recovering parsing
540 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
541 |                       continue retry;
542 |                     }
543 |                   }
544 | 
545 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
546 |                   token = current();
547 | 
548 |                   // Save the throw type.
549 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
550 | 
551 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
552 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
553 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
554 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
555 | 
556 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
557 | 
558 |                     if (description.length() > 0) {
559 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
560 |                     }
561 | 
562 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
563 |                   } else {
564 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
565 |                   }
566 |                   continue retry;
567 | 
568 |                 case PARAM:
569 |                   skipEOLs();
570 |                   token = next();
571 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
572 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
573 |                   type = null;
574 | 
575 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
576 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
577 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
578 | 
579 |                     if (type == null) {
580 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
581 |                       // recovering parsing
582 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
583 |                       continue retry;
584 |                     }
585 |                     skipEOLs();
586 |                     token = next();
587 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
588 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
589 |                   }
590 | 
591 |                   String name = null;
592 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
593 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
594 |                     token = next();
595 |                   }
596 | 
597 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
598 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
599 |                         lineno, charno);
600 |                   } else {
601 |                     name = stream.getString();
602 | 
603 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
604 |                       token = next();
605 | 
606 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter
607 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
608 |                       // system.
609 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
610 |                         token = next();
611 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
612 |                           token = next();
613 |                         }
614 |                       }
615 | 
616 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
617 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
618 |                       } else if (type != null) {
619 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
620 |                         // already.
621 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
622 |                       }
623 |                     }
624 | 
625 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
626 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
627 |                     // for handling properties of params.
628 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
629 |                       name = null;
630 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
631 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
632 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
633 |                             lineno, charno);
634 |                       } else {
635 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
636 |                             lineno, charno);
637 |                       }
638 |                     }
639 |                   }
640 | 
641 |                   if (name == null) {
642 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
643 |                     continue retry;
644 |                   }
645 | 
646 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
647 | 
648 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
649 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
650 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
651 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
652 | 
653 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
654 | 
655 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
656 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
657 |                           paramDescription);
658 |                     }
659 | 
660 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
661 |                   } else {
662 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
663 |                   }
664 |                   continue retry;
665 | 
666 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
667 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
668 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
669 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
670 |                   }
671 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
672 |                   continue retry;
673 | 
674 |                 case PRIVATE:
675 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
676 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
677 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
678 |                   }
679 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
680 |                   continue retry;
681 | 
682 |                 case PROTECTED:
683 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
684 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
685 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
686 |                   }
687 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
688 |                   continue retry;
689 | 
690 |                 case PUBLIC:
691 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
692 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
693 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
694 |                   }
695 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
696 |                   continue retry;
697 | 
698 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
699 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
700 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
701 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
702 |                   }
703 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
704 |                   continue retry;
705 | 
706 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
707 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
708 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
709 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
710 |                   }
711 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
712 |                   continue retry;
713 | 
714 |                 case MODIFIES:
715 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
716 |                   continue retry;
717 | 
718 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
719 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
720 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
721 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
722 |                   }
723 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
724 |                   continue retry;
725 | 
726 |                 case SEE:
727 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
728 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
729 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
730 | 
731 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
732 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
733 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
734 |                     } else {
735 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
736 |                     }
737 | 
738 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
739 |                   } else {
740 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
741 |                   }
742 |                   continue retry;
743 | 
744 |                 case SUPPRESS:
745 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
746 |                   continue retry;
747 | 
748 |                 case TEMPLATE:
749 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
750 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
751 | 
752 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
753 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
754 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
755 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
756 |                       templateTypeName)) {
757 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
758 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
759 |                   }
760 | 
761 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
762 |                   continue retry;
763 | 
764 |                 case VERSION:
765 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
766 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
767 | 
768 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
769 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
770 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
771 |                   } else {
772 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
773 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
774 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
775 |                     }
776 |                   }
777 | 
778 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
779 |                   continue retry;
780 | 
781 |                 case DEFINE:
782 |                 case RETURN:
783 |                 case THIS:
784 |                 case TYPE:
785 |                 case TYPEDEF:
786 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
787 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
788 | 
789 |                   Node typeNode = null;
790 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
791 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
792 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
793 |                     // it as the unknown type.
794 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
795 |                   } else {
796 |                     skipEOLs();
797 |                     token = next();
798 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token, lineno, charno);
799 |                   }
800 | 
801 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
802 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
803 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
804 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
805 |                     }
806 |                   }
807 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
808 | 
809 |                   if (type == null) {
810 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
811 |                     // recovering parsing
812 |                   } else {
813 |                     switch (annotation) {
814 |                       case DEFINE:
815 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
816 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
817 |                               lineno, charno);
818 |                         }
819 |                         break;
820 | 
821 |                       case RETURN:
822 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
823 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
824 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
825 |                           break;
826 |                         }
827 | 
828 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
829 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
830 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
831 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
832 | 
833 |                           String returnDescription =
834 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
835 | 
836 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
837 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
838 |                                 returnDescription);
839 |                           }
840 | 
841 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
842 |                         } else {
843 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
844 |                         }
845 |                         continue retry;
846 | 
847 |                       case THIS:
848 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
849 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
850 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
851 |                         }
852 |                         break;
853 | 
854 |                       case TYPE:
855 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
856 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
857 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
858 |                         }
859 |                         break;
860 | 
861 |                       case TYPEDEF:
862 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
863 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
864 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
865 |                         }
866 |                         break;
867 |                     }
868 | 
869 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
870 |                   }
871 |                   continue retry;
872 |               }
873 |             }
874 |           }
875 |           break;
876 | 
877 |         case EOC:
878 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
879 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
880 |           }
881 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
882 |           return true;
883 | 
884 |         case EOF:
885 |           // discard any accumulated information
886 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
887 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
888 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
889 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
890 |           return false;
891 | 
892 |         case EOL:
893 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
894 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
895 |           }
896 |           token = next();
897 |           continue retry;
898 | 
899 |         default:
900 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
901 |             token = next();
902 |             continue retry;
903 |           } else {
904 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
905 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
906 |             continue retry;
907 |           }
908 |       }
909 | 
910 |       // next token
911 |       token = next();
912 |     }
913 |   }

184 |   @Override
185 |   public int hashCode() {
186 |     int hashCode = 0;
187 |     if (returnType != null) {
188 |       hashCode += returnType.hashCode();
189 |     }
190 |     if (returnTypeInferred) {
191 |       hashCode += 1;
192 |     }
193 |     if (parameters != null) {
194 |       Node param = parameters.getFirstChild();
195 |       while (param != null) {
196 |         JSType paramType = param.getJSType();
197 |         if (paramType != null) {

201 |       }

===== M0237 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 436-488
Documentation:

Method code:
436 |   @Override
437 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
438 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
439 |       return true;
440 |     }
441 | 
442 |     // Union types
443 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
444 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
445 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
446 |       return false;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // record types
450 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
451 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // Interfaces
455 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
456 |     // to the interface instance.
457 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
458 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
459 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
460 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
461 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
462 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
463 |           return true;
464 |         }
465 |       }
466 |     }
467 | 
468 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
469 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
470 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
471 |           return true;
472 |         }
473 |       }
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     // other prototype based objects
477 |     if (that != null) {
478 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
479 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
480 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
481 |         // to avoid guessing.
482 |         return true;
483 |       }
484 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     return false;
488 |   }

===== M0249 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 69-122
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
69 |   @Override
70 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
71 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
72 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
73 |     if (type != null) {
74 |       Node subclass = null;
75 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
76 | 
77 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
78 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
79 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
80 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
81 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
82 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
83 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
84 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
85 |           callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP;
86 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
87 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
88 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
89 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
90 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
91 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
95 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
96 |         // inheritance.
97 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
98 |           return null;
99 |         }
100 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
101 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
102 |             return null;
103 |           }
104 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
105 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
106 |         }
107 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
108 |       }
109 | 
110 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
111 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
112 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
113 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
114 |       if (subclass != null &&
115 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
116 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
117 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
118 |       }
119 |     }
120 | 
121 |     return null;
122 |   }

===== M0255 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 774-873
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the jsdoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
774 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
775 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
776 |         @Nullable String name,
777 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
778 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
779 | 
780 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
781 | 
782 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
783 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
784 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
785 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof FunctionType) {
786 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = (FunctionType) var.getType();
787 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
788 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
789 |             functionType = aliasedType;
790 | 
791 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
792 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
793 |             }
794 |           }
795 |         }
796 |       }
797 | 
798 |       if (functionType == null) {
799 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
800 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
801 |             rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
802 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
803 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
804 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
805 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
806 | 
807 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
808 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
809 | 
810 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
811 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
812 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
813 |             functionType = (FunctionType) type;
814 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
815 |           }
816 |         }
817 | 
818 |         if (functionType == null) {
819 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
820 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
821 |           if (lvalueNode != null &&
822 |               lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
823 |               lvalueNode.isQualifiedName()) {
824 |             Var var = scope.getVar(
825 |                 lvalueNode.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
826 |             if (var != null) {
827 |               ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(var.getType());
828 |               if (ownerType != null) {
829 |                 String propName = lvalueNode.getLastChild().getString();
830 |                 overriddenPropType =
831 |                     findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
832 |               }
833 |             }
834 |           }
835 | 
836 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
837 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
838 |                   scope)
839 |               .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
840 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
841 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
842 |               .inferReturnType(info)
843 |               .inferInheritance(info);
844 | 
845 |           // Infer the context type.
846 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
847 |           if (lvalueNode != null &&
848 |               lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
849 |             Node objNode = lvalueNode.getFirstChild();
850 |             if (objNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
851 |                 objNode.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
852 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getFirstChild());
853 |               searchedForThisType = true;
854 |             } else if (objNode.getType() == Token.THIS) {
855 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getJSType());
856 |               searchedForThisType = true;
857 |             }
858 |           }
859 | 
860 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
861 |             builder.inferThisType(info, (Node) null);
862 |           }
863 | 
864 |           functionType = builder
865 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
866 |               .inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(fnBlock)
867 |               .buildAndRegister();
868 |         }
869 |       }
870 | 
871 |       // all done
872 |       return functionType;
873 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
786 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
787 |     switch (n.getType()) {
788 |       case Token.DO:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
790 |       case Token.FOR:
791 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
792 |           return n;
793 |         }
794 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
795 |       case Token.LABEL:
796 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
797 |       default:
798 |         return n;
799 |     }
800 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 234-277
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
234 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
237 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
258 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
268 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
269 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "

===== M0268 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1026-1127
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1026 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1027 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1028 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1029 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1030 |     // already.
1031 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1032 |       return;
1033 |     }
1034 | 
1035 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1036 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1037 |         superClass.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1038 |     // For interface
1039 |     boolean superInterfacesHasProperty = false;
1040 |     if (ctorType.isInterface()) {
1041 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1042 |         superInterfacesHasProperty =
1043 |           superInterfacesHasProperty || interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName);
1044 |       }
1045 |     }
1046 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1047 | 
1048 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1049 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1050 |       for (JSType implementedInterface :
1051 |           ctorType.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
1052 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1053 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1054 |           continue;
1055 |         }
1056 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1057 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1058 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1059 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1060 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1061 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty || interfaceHasProperty;
1062 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && !declaredOverride &&
1063 |             interfaceHasProperty) {
1064 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1065 |           // property
1066 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1067 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1068 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1069 |         }
1070 |       }
1071 |     }
1072 | 
1073 |     if (!declaredOverride && !superClassHasProperty
1074 |         && !superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1075 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1076 |       return;
1077 |     }
1078 | 
1079 |     JSType topInstanceType = superClassHasProperty ?
1080 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1081 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1082 |         !declaredOverride && superClassHasProperty) {
1083 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1084 |       // property
1085 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1086 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1087 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1088 |     }
1089 |     if (!declaredOverride) {
1090 |       // there's no @override to check
1091 |       return;
1092 |     }
1093 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1094 |     if (superClassHasProperty) {
1095 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1096 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1097 |           superClass.getPrototype().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1098 |       if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superClassPropType)) {
1099 |         compiler.report(
1100 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1101 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1102 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1103 |       }
1104 |     } else if (superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1105 |       // there is an super interface property
1106 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1107 |         if (interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName)) {
1108 |           JSType superPropertyType =
1109 |             interfaceType.getPropertyType(propertyName);
1110 |           if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superPropertyType)) {
1111 |             topInstanceType = interfaceType.getConstructor().
1112 |                 getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName);
1113 |             compiler.report(
1114 |                 t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1115 |                     propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1116 |                     superPropertyType.toString(),
1117 |                     propertyType.toString()));
1118 |           }
1119 |         }
1120 |       }
1121 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty) {
1122 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1123 |       compiler.report(
1124 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1125 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1126 |     }
1127 |   }

===== M0276 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1526-1561
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1526 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1527 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1528 | 
1529 |     skipEOLs();
1530 | 
1531 |     boolean restArg = false;
1532 |     token = next();
1533 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1534 |       token = next();
1535 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1536 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1537 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1538 |       }
1539 |       restArg = true;
1540 |     }
1541 | 
1542 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1543 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1544 |       skipEOLs();
1545 |       if (restArg) {
1546 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1547 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1548 |         next();
1549 |         skipEOLs();
1550 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1551 |       }
1552 | 
1553 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1554 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1555 |       } else {
1556 |         next();
1557 |       }
1558 |     }
1559 | 
1560 |     return typeNode;
1561 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 832-874
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
832 |   @Override
833 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
834 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
835 |       return true;
836 |     }
837 | 
838 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
839 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
840 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
841 |         return true;
842 |       }
843 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
844 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
845 |         return false;
846 |       }
847 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
848 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
849 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
850 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
851 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
852 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
853 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
854 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
855 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
856 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
857 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
858 | 
859 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
860 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
861 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
862 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
863 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
864 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
865 | 
866 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
867 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
868 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
869 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
870 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
871 |     }
872 | 
873 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
874 |   }

===== M0313 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

===== M0319 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1874-1898
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1874 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1875 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1876 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1877 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1878 |             type.isUnknownType());
1879 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1880 |     if (info != null) {
1881 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1882 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1883 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1884 |         type = infoType;
1885 |       }
1886 | 
1887 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1888 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1889 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1890 |         compiler.report(
1891 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1892 |       }
1893 |     }
1894 | 
1895 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1896 |       n.setJSType(type);
1897 |     }
1898 |   }

/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */
Method code:
228 |   public int getMinArguments() {
229 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
230 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position
231 |     // of the last required parameter.

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 506-562
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   */
Method code:
506 |   void expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, Node n, Node parent, Var var,
507 |       String variableName, JSType newType) {
508 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
509 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ||
510 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
511 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
512 |       if (info == null) {
513 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
514 |       }
515 |       allowDupe =
516 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
517 |     }
518 | 
519 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
520 | 
521 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
522 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
523 |     // compilation process.
524 |     if (varType != null &&
525 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
526 |         newType != null &&
527 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
528 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
529 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
530 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
531 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
532 |       // native type.
533 |       if (var.input == null) {
534 |         n.setJSType(varType);
535 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
536 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
537 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
538 |           }
539 |         } else {
540 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
541 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
542 |         }
543 |       } else {
544 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
545 |         // match the original type.
546 |         //
547 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
548 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
549 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
550 |               var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) ||
551 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
552 |           if (shouldReport) {
553 |             compiler.report(
554 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
555 |                     variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
556 |                     String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
557 |                     varType.toString()));
558 |           }
559 |         }
560 |       }
561 |     }
562 |   }

===== M0332 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 817-861
Documentation:

Method code:
817 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
818 |       Node callNode) {
819 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
820 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
821 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
822 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
823 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
824 |       return scope;
825 |     }
826 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
827 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
828 |       return scope;
829 |     }
830 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
831 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
832 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
833 |     if (assertedType == null) {
834 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
835 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
836 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
837 |         if (type != narrowed) {
838 |           scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
839 |           redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
840 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
841 |         }
842 |       } else if (assertedNode.getType() == Token.AND ||
843 |                  assertedNode.getType() == Token.OR) {
844 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
845 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
846 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
847 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
848 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
849 |       }
850 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
851 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
852 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
853 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
854 |       if (type != narrowed) {
855 |         scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
856 |         redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
857 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
858 |       }
859 |     }
860 |     return scope;
861 |   }

===== M0334 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 150-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
150 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
151 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
152 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
153 |       case Token.BLOCK:
154 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
155 |       case Token.TRY:
156 |       case Token.FINALLY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1924-1926
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1924 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1925 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1926 |   }
