===== M0341 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 693-729
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
693 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
694 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
695 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
696 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
697 | 
698 |     if (existingType != null) {
699 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
700 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
701 |         FunctionType existingFn =
702 |             isInstanceObject ?
703 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
704 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
705 | 
706 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
707 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
708 |         }
709 | 
710 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
711 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
712 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
713 |         }
714 | 
715 |         return existingFn;
716 |       } else {
717 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
718 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
719 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
720 |       }
721 |     }
722 | 
723 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
724 | 
725 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
726 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
727 |     }
728 |     return fnType;
729 |   }

===== M0348 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1544-1590
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1544 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1545 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1546 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1547 | 
1548 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1549 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1550 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1551 |       return;
1552 |     }
1553 | 
1554 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1555 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1556 |     if (childType instanceof FunctionType) {
1557 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) childType;
1558 | 
1559 |       boolean isExtern = false;
1560 |       JSDocInfo functionJSDocInfo = functionType.getJSDocInfo();
1561 |       if(functionJSDocInfo != null) {
1562 |         String sourceName = functionJSDocInfo.getSourceName();
1563 |         CompilerInput functionSource = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
1564 |         isExtern = functionSource.isExtern();
1565 |       }
1566 | 
1567 |       // Non-native constructors should not be called directly
1568 |       // unless they specify a return type and are defined
1569 |       // in an extern.
1570 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1571 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType() &&
1572 |           (functionType.getReturnType().isUnknownType() ||
1573 |            functionType.getReturnType().isVoidType() ||
1574 |            !isExtern)) {
1575 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1576 |       }
1577 | 
1578 |       // Functions with explcit 'this' types must be called in a GETPROP
1579 |       // or GETELEM.
1580 | 
1581 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1582 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1583 |     } else {
1584 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1585 |     }
1586 | 
1587 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1588 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1589 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1590 |   }

/**
   * Informally, a function is represented by
   * {@code function (params): returnType} where the {@code params} is a comma
   * separated list of types, the first one being a special
   * {@code this:T} if the function expects a known type for {@code this}.
   */
Method code:
773 |   @Override
774 |   public String toString() {
775 |     if (this == registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE)) {
776 |       return "Function";
777 |     }
778 | 
779 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(32);
780 |     b.append("function (");
781 |     int paramNum = call.parameters.getChildCount();
782 |     boolean hasKnownTypeOfThis = !typeOfThis.isUnknownType();
783 |     if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
784 |       if (isConstructor()) {
785 |         b.append("new:");
786 |       } else {
787 |         b.append("this:");
788 |       }
789 |       b.append(typeOfThis.toString());
790 |     }
791 |     if (paramNum > 0) {
792 |       if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
793 |         b.append(", ");
794 |       }
795 |       Node p = call.parameters.getFirstChild();
796 |       if (p.isVarArgs()) {
797 |         appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
798 |       } else {
799 |         b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
800 |       }
801 |       p = p.getNext();
802 |       while (p != null) {
803 |         b.append(", ");
804 |         if (p.isVarArgs()) {
805 |           appendVarArgsString(b, p.getJSType());
806 |         } else {
807 |           b.append(p.getJSType().toString());
808 |         }
809 |         p = p.getNext();
810 |       }
811 |     }
812 |     b.append("): ");
813 |     b.append(call.returnType);
814 |     return b.toString();
815 |   }

===== M0367 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:

Method code:
252 |   @Override
253 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
254 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
255 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
256 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
257 |         return true;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
261 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
262 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
263 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
264 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
265 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
266 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
267 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
268 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
269 |         return false;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
273 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
274 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
275 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
276 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
277 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
278 |             return false;
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 | 
282 |         return true;
283 |       }
284 | 
285 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
286 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
287 | 
288 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
289 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
290 |           return false;
291 |         }
292 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
293 |       }
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |       return true;
300 |     }
301 |     return false;
302 |   }

===== M0378 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0384 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 239-290
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
239 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
240 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
241 |     if (oldType == null) {
242 |       return this;
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
246 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
247 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
248 |       // Not a function literal.
249 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
250 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
251 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
252 |       }
253 |     } else {
254 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
255 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
256 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
257 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
258 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
259 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
260 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
261 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
262 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
263 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
264 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
265 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
266 | 
267 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
268 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
269 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
270 | 
271 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
272 |           // arguments.
273 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
274 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
275 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
276 |           }
277 |         } else {
278 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
279 |               paramBuilder,
280 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
281 |               warnedAboutArgList,
282 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
283 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
284 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
285 |         }
286 |       }
287 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
288 |     }
289 |     return this;
290 |   }

===== M0395 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 242-268
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
242 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
243 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
244 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
245 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
246 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
247 | 
248 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
249 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
250 |       // if (this.x == null) {
251 |       //   this.initializeX();
252 |       //   this.x.foo();
253 |       // }
254 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
255 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
256 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
257 |       //
258 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
259 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
260 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
261 |         return true;
262 |       }
263 | 
264 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
265 |       return false;
266 |     }
267 |     return true;
268 |   }

===== M0399 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectIndexMatch(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 310-326
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be addressed with GETELEM syntax,
   * and that the second type is the right type for an index into the
   * first type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param objType The type of the left side of the GETELEM.
   * @param indexType The type inside the brackets of the GETELEM.
   */
Method code:
310 |   void expectIndexMatch(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType objType,
311 |       JSType indexType) {
312 |     if (objType.isUnknownType()) {
313 |       expectStringOrNumber(t, n, indexType, "property access");
314 |     } else if (objType.toObjectType() != null &&
315 |         objType.toObjectType().getIndexType() != null) {
316 |       expectCanAssignTo(t, n, indexType, objType.toObjectType().getIndexType(),
317 |           "restricted index type");
318 |     } else if (objType.isArrayType()) {
319 |       expectNumber(t, n, indexType, "array access");
320 |     } else if (objType.matchesObjectContext()) {
321 |       expectString(t, n, indexType, "property access");
322 |     } else {
323 |       mismatch(t, n, "only arrays or objects can be accessed",
324 |           objType, typeRegistry.createUnionType(ARRAY_TYPE, OBJECT_TYPE));
325 |     }
326 |   }

===== M0400 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.differsFrom(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 875-882
Documentation:
/**
   * Whether this type is meaningfully different from {@code that} type.
   * This is a trickier check than pure equality, because it has to properly
   * handle unknown types.
   *
   * @see <a href="http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_RpSv3HjpEw">Unknown
   *     unknowns</a>
   */
Method code:
875 |   public boolean differsFrom(JSType that) {
876 |     // if there are no unknowns, just use normal equality.
877 |     if (!this.isUnknownType() && !that.isUnknownType()) {
878 |       return !this.isEquivalentTo(that);
879 |     }
880 |     // otherwise, they're different iff one is unknown and the other is not.
881 |     return this.isUnknownType() ^ that.isUnknownType();
882 |   }

===== M0405 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1020-1131
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1020 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1021 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1022 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1023 | 
1024 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.getType() == Token.NAME && scope.isGlobal();
1025 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1026 |           isGlobalVar &&
1027 |           (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
1028 |            parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
1029 | 
1030 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1031 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1032 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1033 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1034 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1035 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1036 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1037 | 
1038 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1039 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1040 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1041 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1042 |         }
1043 |       }
1044 | 
1045 |       // declared in closest scope?
1046 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1047 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1048 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1049 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1050 |       } else {
1051 |         if (!inferred) {
1052 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1053 |         }
1054 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
1055 |         boolean isExtern = input.isExtern();
1056 |         Var newVar =
1057 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1058 | 
1059 |         if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1060 |           ObjectType globalThis =
1061 |               typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1062 |           if (inferred) {
1063 |             globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1064 |                 type == null ?
1065 |                     getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1066 |                     type,
1067 |                 isExtern, n);
1068 |           } else {
1069 |             globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, isExtern, n);
1070 |           }
1071 |         }
1072 | 
1073 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1074 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1075 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1076 |               (initialValue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT ||
1077 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1078 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1079 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1080 |           }
1081 |         }
1082 | 
1083 |         // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1084 |         if (type instanceof FunctionType &&
1085 |             // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1086 |             !type.isEmptyType()) {
1087 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
1088 |           if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1089 |               !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1090 |             // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1091 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1092 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
1093 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), input,
1094 |                 /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1095 |                 superClassCtor == null ||
1096 |                 superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1097 |                     getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1098 | 
1099 |             // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1100 |             // it constructs itself.
1101 |             if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1102 |                 !isExtern &&
1103 |                 // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1104 |                 // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1105 |                 // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1106 |                 // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1107 |                 // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1108 |                 // this.
1109 |                 variableName.equals(
1110 |                     fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1111 |               compiler.report(
1112 |                   JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1113 |                       fnType.isConstructor() ?
1114 |                           CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1115 |                       variableName));
1116 |             }
1117 |           }
1118 |         }
1119 |       }
1120 | 
1121 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1122 |           && type instanceof FunctionType
1123 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1124 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1125 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1126 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1127 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1128 |         globalThisCtor
1129 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn(((FunctionType) type).getInstanceType());
1130 |       }
1131 |     }

===== M0412 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.getBestLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1778-1792
Documentation:
/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
1778 |   private static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
1779 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1780 |     int parentType = parent.getType();
1781 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
1782 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
1783 |       return n.getFirstChild();
1784 |     } else if (parentType == Token.NAME) {
1785 |       return parent;
1786 |     } else if (parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
1787 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
1788 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
1789 |       return parent;
1790 |     }
1791 |     return null;
1792 |   }

1011 |     @Override
1012 |     /**
1013 |      * Returns a node comparator based on the pre-order traversal of the AST.
1014 |      * @param isForward x 'before' y in the pre-order traversal implies
1015 |      * x 'less than' y (if true) and x 'greater than' y (if false).
1016 |      */
1017 |     public Comparator<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> getOptionalNodeComparator(
1018 |         boolean isForward) {

1021 |           @Override
1022 |           public int compare(
1023 |               DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n1, DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n2) {
1024 |             return getPosition(n1) - getPosition(n2);
1025 |           }
1026 |         };
1027 |       } else {
1028 |         return new Comparator<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>() {
1029 |           @Override
1030 |           public int compare(
1031 |               DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n1, DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n2) {
1032 |             return getPosition(n2) - getPosition(n1);
1033 |           }
1034 |         };
1035 |       }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.getType(StaticScope<JSType>,String,String,int,int)

Original range: 841-853
Documentation:
/**
   * Looks up a type by name. To allow for forward references to types, an
   * unrecognized string has to be bound to a NamedType object that will be
   * resolved later.
   *
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name resolution.
   * @param jsTypeName The name string.
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file where this reference appears.
   * @param lineno The line number of the reference.
   * @return a NamedType if the string argument is not one of the known types,
   *     otherwise the corresponding JSType object.
   */
Method code:
841 |   public JSType getType(StaticScope<JSType> scope, String jsTypeName,
842 |       String sourceName, int lineno, int charno) {
843 |     JSType type = getType(jsTypeName);
844 |     if (type == null) {
845 |       // TODO(user): Each instance should support named type creation using
846 |       // interning.
847 |       NamedType namedType =
848 |           new NamedType(this, jsTypeName, sourceName, lineno, charno);
849 |       unresolvedNamedTypes.put(scope, namedType);
850 |       type = namedType;
851 |     }
852 |     return type;
853 |   }

/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
467 |   @Override
468 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
469 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
470 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
471 |     if (unknown) {
472 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
473 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
474 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
475 |         unknown = false;
476 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
478 |             unknown = true;
479 |             break;
480 |           }
481 |         }
482 |       } else {
483 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
484 |       }
485 |     }
486 |     return unknown;
487 |   }

===== M0444 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0455 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 188-300
Documentation:

Method code:
188 |   @Override
189 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
190 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
191 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
192 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
193 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
194 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
195 |     // worthwhile.
196 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
197 |     Node condition = null;
198 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
199 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
200 | 
201 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
202 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
203 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
204 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
205 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
206 | 
207 |       switch (branch) {
208 |         case ON_TRUE:
209 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
210 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
211 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
212 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
213 | 
214 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
215 | 
216 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
217 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
218 |             }
219 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
220 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
221 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
222 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
223 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
224 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
225 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
226 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
227 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
228 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
229 |                 }
230 |               }
231 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(), iterKeyType);
232 |             }
233 |             newScope = informed;
234 |             break;
235 |           }
236 | 
237 |           // FALL THROUGH
238 | 
239 |         case ON_FALSE:
240 |           if (condition == null) {
241 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
242 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
243 |               condition = source;
244 | 
245 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
246 |               // of the loop.
247 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
248 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
249 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
250 |               }
251 |             }
252 |           }
253 | 
254 |           if (condition != null) {
255 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
256 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
257 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
258 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
259 |               // scope on false.
260 |               //
261 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
262 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
263 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
264 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
265 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
266 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
267 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
268 | 
269 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
270 |               // of the loop.
271 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
272 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
273 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
274 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
275 |               }
276 |               newScope =
277 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
278 |                       condition,
279 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
280 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
281 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
282 |             } else {
283 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
284 |               // of the loop.
285 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
286 |                 conditionFlowScope =
287 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
288 |               }
289 |               newScope =
290 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
291 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
292 |             }
293 |           }
294 |           break;
295 |       }
296 | 
297 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
298 |     }
299 |     return result;
300 |   }

Original range: 119-155
Documentation:
// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
119 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
120 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
121 |                 Scope functionScope,
122 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap,
123 |                 Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1717-1750
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1717 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1718 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1719 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1720 |     }
1721 | 
1722 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1723 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1724 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1725 |       skipEOLs();
1726 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1727 |         next();
1728 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1729 |       }
1730 |     }
1731 | 
1732 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName);
1733 | 
1734 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1735 |       next();
1736 |       skipEOLs();
1737 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1738 |       if (memberType != null) {
1739 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1740 | 
1741 |         skipEOLs();
1742 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1743 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1744 |         }
1745 | 
1746 |         next();
1747 |       }
1748 |     }
1749 |     return typeNameNode;
1750 |   }

/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1211 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1212 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1213 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1214 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1215 |       if (relationship != null) {
1216 |         ObjectType superClass = ObjectType.cast(
1217 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.superclassName));
1218 |         ObjectType subClass = ObjectType.cast(
1219 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.subclassName));
1220 |         if (superClass != null && subClass != null) {
1221 |           FunctionType superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1222 |           FunctionType subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1223 | 
1224 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS) {
1225 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1226 |           }
1227 | 
1228 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1229 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1230 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1231 |           }
1232 |         }
1233 |       }
1234 | 
1235 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1236 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1237 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1238 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1239 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1240 |         if (objectType != null) {
1241 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1242 | 
1243 |           if (functionType != null) {
1244 |             FunctionType getterType =
1245 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1246 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1247 |                 objectType);
1248 |           }
1249 |         }
1250 |       }
1251 | 
1252 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1253 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1254 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1255 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1256 |       }
1257 | 
1258 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1259 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
1260 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1261 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1262 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1263 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1264 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1265 |         } else {

1267 |                   CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));

===== M0475 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleFor(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 395-430
Documentation:

Method code:
395 |   private void handleFor(Node forNode) {
396 |     if (forNode.getChildCount() == 4) {
397 |       // We have for (init; cond; iter) { body }
398 |       Node init = forNode.getFirstChild();
399 |       Node cond = init.getNext();
400 |       Node iter = cond.getNext();
401 |       Node body = iter.getNext();
402 |       // After initialization, we transfer to the FOR which is in charge of
403 |       // checking the condition (for the first time).
404 |       createEdge(init, Branch.UNCOND, forNode);
405 |       // The edge that transfer control to the beginning of the loop body.
406 |       createEdge(forNode, Branch.ON_TRUE, computeFallThrough(body));
407 |       // The edge to end of the loop.
408 |       createEdge(forNode, Branch.ON_FALSE,
409 |           computeFollowNode(forNode, this));
410 |       // The end of the body will have a unconditional branch to our iter
411 |       // (handled by calling computeFollowNode of the last instruction of the
412 |       // body. Our iter will jump to the forNode again to another condition
413 |       // check.
414 |       createEdge(iter, Branch.UNCOND, forNode);
415 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(init, init);
416 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(forNode, cond);
417 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(iter, iter);
418 |     } else {
419 |       // We have for (item in collection) { body }
420 |       Node item = forNode.getFirstChild();
421 |       Node collection = item.getNext();
422 |       Node body = collection.getNext();
423 |       // The edge that transfer control to the beginning of the loop body.
424 |       createEdge(forNode, Branch.ON_TRUE, computeFallThrough(body));
425 |       // The edge to end of the loop.
426 |       createEdge(forNode, Branch.ON_FALSE,
427 |           computeFollowNode(forNode, this));
428 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(forNode, collection);
429 |     }
430 |   }

302 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
303 |     switch (n.getType()) {

318 |             .createChildFlowScope();
319 |         break;
320 | 
321 |       case Token.OR:
322 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
323 |             .createChildFlowScope();
324 |         break;
325 | 
326 |       case Token.HOOK:
327 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
328 |         break;
329 | 
330 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
331 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
332 |         break;
333 | 
334 |       case Token.CALL:
335 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
336 |         break;
337 | 
338 |       case Token.NEW:
339 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
340 |         break;
341 | 
342 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
343 |       case Token.ADD:
344 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
345 |         break;
346 | 
347 |       case Token.POS:
348 |       case Token.NEG:
349 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
350 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
351 |         break;
352 | 
353 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
354 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
355 |         break;
356 |

358 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
359 |         break;

362 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
363 |       case Token.LSH:
364 |       case Token.RSH:
365 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
366 |       case Token.URSH:

368 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
369 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
370 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
371 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
372 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
373 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
374 |       case Token.DIV:
375 |       case Token.MOD:

377 |       case Token.BITXOR:
378 |       case Token.BITOR:
379 |       case Token.MUL:
380 |       case Token.SUB:
381 |       case Token.DEC:

384 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
385 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
386 |         break;

389 |       case Token.GET_REF:

392 |         break;

395 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
396 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
397 |         break;
398 | 
399 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
400 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
401 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
402 |         break;
403 | 
404 |       case Token.DELPROP:
405 |       case Token.LT:
406 |       case Token.LE:
407 |       case Token.GT:
408 |       case Token.GE:
409 |       case Token.NOT:
410 |       case Token.EQ:
411 |       case Token.NE:
412 |       case Token.SHEQ:

415 |       case Token.IN:

418 |         break;

427 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
428 |         }
429 |         break;

432 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
433 |         break;
434 | 
435 |       case Token.VAR:
436 |       case Token.RETURN:
437 |       case Token.THROW:
438 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.CATCH:
442 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
443 |         break;
444 |     }
445 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
446 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
447 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
448 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
449 |

451 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
452 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.

454 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
455 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
456 |         }

463 |   }

===== M0503 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 420-436
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
420 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
421 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
422 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
423 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
424 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
425 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
426 |         String inputName = input.getName();
427 | 
428 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
429 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
430 |         // will get an error down the line.
431 |         inputs.add(input);
432 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
433 |       }
434 |     }
435 |     return inputs;
436 |   }

===== M0504 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 632-671
Documentation:

Method code:
632 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
633 |     String varName = n.getString();
634 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
635 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
636 |     if (value != null) {
637 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
638 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
639 |           getJSType(value));
640 |       return scope;
641 |     } else {
642 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
643 |       if (var != null) {
644 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
645 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
646 | 
647 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
648 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
649 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
650 |         boolean unflowable =
651 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
652 | 
653 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
654 |         // inferred variable.
655 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
656 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
657 |             isInferred &&
658 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
659 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
660 | 
661 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
662 |           type = var.getType();
663 |           if (type == null) {
664 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
665 |           }
666 |         }
667 |       }
668 |     }
669 |     n.setJSType(type);
670 |     return scope;
671 |   }

===== M0507 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0511 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1133-1155
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, find out whether the unknown
   * type is a supertype of the current type.
   */
Method code:
1133 |   private static boolean hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType ctor) {
1134 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1135 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!ctor.isUnknownType());
1136 | 
1137 |     // The type system should notice inheritance cycles on its own
1138 |     // and break the cycle.
1139 |     while (true) {
1140 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
1141 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
1142 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
1143 |         return false;
1144 |       }
1145 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType() ||
1146 |           maybeSuperInstanceType.isEmptyType()) {
1147 |         return true;
1148 |       }
1149 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
1150 |       if (ctor == null) {
1151 |         return false;
1152 |       }
1153 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1154 |     }
1155 |   }

===== M0521 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.caseEquality(Node,Node,FlowScope,Function<TypePair, TypePair>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 265-304
Documentation:

Method code:
265 |   private FlowScope caseEquality(Node left, Node right, FlowScope blindScope,
266 |       Function<TypePair, TypePair> merging) {
267 |     // left type
268 |     JSType leftType = getTypeIfRefinable(left, blindScope);
269 |     boolean leftIsRefineable;
270 |     if (leftType != null) {
271 |       leftIsRefineable = true;
272 |     } else {
273 |       leftIsRefineable = false;
274 |       leftType = left.getJSType();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     // right type
278 |     JSType rightType = getTypeIfRefinable(right, blindScope);
279 |     boolean rightIsRefineable;
280 |     if (rightType != null) {
281 |       rightIsRefineable = true;
282 |     } else {
283 |       rightIsRefineable = false;
284 |       rightType = right.getJSType();
285 |     }
286 | 
287 |     // merged types
288 |     TypePair merged = merging.apply(new TypePair(leftType, rightType));
289 | 
290 |     // creating new scope
291 |     if (merged != null &&
292 |         ((leftIsRefineable && merged.typeA != null) ||
293 |          (rightIsRefineable && merged.typeB != null))) {
294 |       FlowScope informed = blindScope.createChildFlowScope();
295 |       if (leftIsRefineable && merged.typeA != null) {
296 |         declareNameInScope(informed, left, merged.typeA);
297 |       }
298 |       if (rightIsRefineable && merged.typeB != null) {
299 |         declareNameInScope(informed, right, merged.typeB);
300 |       }
301 |       return informed;
302 |     }
303 |     return blindScope;
304 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1160 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1162 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1163 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1164 |       } else {
1165 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1166 |       }
1167 |     }
1168 |     return builder.build();
1169 |   }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 630-672
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
630 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
631 |     if (returnType == null) {
632 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
633 |     }
634 | 
635 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
636 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
637 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
638 |     }
639 | 
640 |     FunctionType fnType;
641 |     if (isConstructor) {
642 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
643 |     } else if (isInterface) {
644 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(fnName, sourceNode);
645 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
646 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
647 |       }
648 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
649 |     } else {
650 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
651 |           .withName(fnName)
652 |           .withSourceNode(sourceNode)
653 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
654 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
655 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
656 |           .withTemplateName(templateTypeName)
657 |           .build();
658 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
659 |     }
660 | 
661 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
662 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
663 |     }
664 | 
665 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
666 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
667 |     }
668 | 
669 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeName();
670 | 
671 |     return fnType;
672 |   }

Method code:
102 |   void inferTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Scope scope) {
103 |     TypeInference typeInference =
104 |         new TypeInference(
105 |             compiler, computeCfg(n), reverseInterpreter, scope,
106 |             assertionFunctionsMap, getUnflowableVars(scope));

113 |

115 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, DATAFLOW_ERROR));

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.getTypeIfRefinable(Node,FlowScope)

/**
   * Returns the type of a node in the given scope if the node corresponds to a
   * name whose type is capable of being refined.
   * @return The current type of the node if it can be refined, null otherwise.
   */
Method code:
116 |   JSType getTypeIfRefinable(Node node, FlowScope scope) {
117 |     switch (node.getType()) {
118 |       case Token.NAME:
119 |         StaticSlot<JSType> nameVar = scope.getSlot(node.getString());
120 |         if (nameVar != null) {
121 |           JSType nameVarType = nameVar.getType();
122 |           if (nameVarType == null) {
123 |             nameVarType = node.getJSType();
124 |           }
125 |           return nameVarType;
126 |         }
127 |         return null;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.GETPROP:
130 |         String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
131 |         if (qualifiedName == null) {
132 |           return null;
133 |         }
134 |         StaticSlot<JSType> propVar = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
135 |         JSType propVarType = null;
136 |         if (propVar != null) {
137 |           propVarType = propVar.getType();
138 |         }
139 |         if (propVarType == null) {
140 |           propVarType = node.getJSType();
141 |         }
142 |         if (propVarType == null) {
143 |           propVarType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
144 |         }
145 |         return propVarType;
146 |     }
147 |     return null;
148 |   }

===== M0567 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 682-779
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
682 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
683 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
684 |     /*
685 |      * This is the case where:
686 |      *
687 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
688 |      * the script.
689 |      *
690 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
691 |      * the caller of the function.
692 |      *
693 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
694 |      * back to the caller of the function.
695 |      *
696 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
697 |      * asked to traverse.
698 |      *
699 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
700 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
701 |      */
702 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
703 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
704 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
705 |       return null;
706 |     }
707 | 
708 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
709 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
710 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
711 |       case Token.IF:
712 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
715 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
716 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
717 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
718 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
719 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
720 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
721 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
722 |           } else {
723 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
724 |           }
725 |         } else {
726 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
727 |         }
728 |         break;
729 |       case Token.FOR:
730 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
731 |           return parent;
732 |         } else {
733 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
734 |         }
735 |       case Token.WHILE:
736 |       case Token.DO:
737 |         return parent;
738 |       case Token.TRY:
739 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
740 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
743 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // CATCH block.
747 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
748 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
749 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
750 |           } else {
751 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
752 |           }
753 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
754 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
755 |           if (cfa != null) {
756 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
757 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
758 |             }
759 |           }
760 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
761 |         }
762 |     }
763 | 
764 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
765 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
766 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
767 | 
768 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
769 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
770 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
774 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
775 |     } else {
776 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
777 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
778 |     }
779 |   }

868 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1219-1248
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1219 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1220 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1221 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1222 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1223 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1224 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1225 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1226 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1227 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1228 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1229 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1230 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1231 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1232 |       return false;
1233 |     }
1234 | 
1235 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1236 |     if (type == null) {
1237 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1238 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1239 |       if (var != null) {
1240 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1241 |         if (varType != null) {
1242 |           type = varType;
1243 |         }
1244 |       }
1245 |     }
1246 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1247 |     return true;
1248 |   }

===== M0592 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1368 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1369 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1370 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1371 |     // merge to fix this.
1372 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1373 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1374 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1375 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1376 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1377 | 
1378 |       if (value != null) {
1379 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1380 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1381 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1382 | 
1383 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1384 |         if (info == null) {
1385 |           info = varInfo;
1386 |         }
1387 |         if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1388 |           // var.getType() can never be null, this would indicate a bug in the
1389 |           // scope creation logic.
1390 |           checkEnumInitializer(
1391 |               t, value,
1392 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
1393 |         } else if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1394 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1395 |         } else {
1396 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1397 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1398 |         }
1399 |       }
1400 |     }
1401 |   }

/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
581 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {
582 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
583 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
584 |       return that.getLeastSupertype(this);
585 |     }
586 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
587 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

===== M0620 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 730-756
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
730 |   @Override
731 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
732 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
733 |       return false;
734 |     }
735 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
736 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
737 |       return false;
738 |     }
739 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
740 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
741 |         return this == that;
742 |       }
743 |       return false;
744 |     }
745 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
747 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
748 |       }
749 |       return false;
750 |     }
751 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
752 |       return false;
753 |     }
754 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
755 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
756 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1595-1629
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1595 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1596 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1597 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1598 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1599 | 
1600 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1601 |     int ordinal = 0;
1602 |     Node parameter = null;
1603 |     Node argument = null;
1604 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1605 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1606 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1607 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1608 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1609 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1610 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1611 |       }
1612 |       argument = arguments.next();
1613 |       ordinal++;
1614 | 
1615 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1616 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1617 |     }
1618 | 
1619 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1620 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1621 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1622 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1623 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1624 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1625 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1626 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1627 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1628 |     }
1629 |   }
