Original range: 349-396
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
349 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
350 |     if (info != null) {
351 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
352 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
353 | 
354 |       // base type
355 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
356 |         if (isConstructor) {
357 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
358 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
359 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
360 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
361 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
362 |           }
363 |         } else {
364 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
365 |         }
366 |       }
367 | 
368 |       // implemented interfaces
369 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
370 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
371 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
372 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
373 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
374 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
375 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
376 |           }
377 |         }
378 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
379 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
380 |       }
381 | 
382 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
383 |       if (isInterface) {
384 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
385 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
386 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
387 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
388 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
389 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
390 |           }
391 |         }
392 |       }
393 |     }
394 | 
395 |     return this;
396 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1452-1482
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1452 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1453 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1454 |         Node n = stub.node;
1455 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1456 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1457 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1458 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1459 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1460 | 
1461 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1462 |           continue;
1463 |         }
1464 | 
1465 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1466 |         // in the type registry.
1467 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1468 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1469 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1470 | 
1471 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1472 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1473 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1474 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1475 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1476 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern, n);
1477 |         } else {
1478 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1479 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1480 |         }
1481 |       }
1482 |     }

===== M0652 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
820 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
821 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
822 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
823 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
824 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
825 |           return;
826 |         }
827 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
828 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
829 | 
830 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
831 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
832 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
835 |           }
836 |         } else { // Has a catch.
837 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
838 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
839 |             return;
840 |           } else {
841 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
842 |           }
843 |         }
844 |         lastJump = handler;
845 |       }
846 |     }
847 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)

Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

===== M0662 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 545-590
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
545 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
546 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
547 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
548 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
549 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
550 |     if (objectType == null) {
551 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
552 |     } else {
553 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
554 |         return;
555 |       }
556 | 
557 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
558 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
559 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
560 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
561 |         //
562 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
563 |         //    inferred type.
564 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
565 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
566 |         //    define it.
567 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
568 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
569 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
570 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
571 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
572 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
573 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
574 |                 propName, rightType, false, getprop);
575 |           } else {
576 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
577 |                 propName, rightType, false, getprop);
578 |           }
579 |         } else {
580 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS &&
581 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
582 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
583 |                 propName, rightType, false, getprop);
584 |           } else {
585 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
586 |           }
587 |         }
588 |       }
589 |     }
590 |   }

443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {

454 |       return false;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferThisType(JSDocInfo,Node)

Original range: 423-455
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param info The JSDocInfo for this function.
   * @param owner The node for the object whose prototype "owns" this function.
   *     For example, {@code A} in the expression {@code A.prototype.foo}. May
   *     be null to indicate that this is not a prototype property.
   */
Method code:
423 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferThisType(JSDocInfo info,
424 |       @Nullable Node owner) {
425 |     ObjectType maybeThisType = null;
426 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
427 |       maybeThisType = ObjectType.cast(
428 |           info.getThisType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
429 |     }
430 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
431 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
432 |       thisType.setValidator(new ThisTypeValidator());
433 |     } else if (owner != null &&
434 |                (info == null || !info.hasType())) {
435 |       // If the function is of the form:
436 |       // x.prototype.y = function() {}
437 |       // then we can assume "x" is the @this type. On the other hand,
438 |       // if it's of the form:
439 |       // /** @type {Function} */ x.prototype.y;
440 |       // then we should not give it a @this type.
441 |       String ownerTypeName = owner.getQualifiedName();
442 |       Var ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerTypeName);
443 |       JSType ownerType = ownerVar == null ? null : ownerVar.getType();
444 |       FunctionType ownerFnType = ownerType instanceof FunctionType ?
445 |           (FunctionType) ownerType : null;
446 |       ObjectType instType =
447 |           ownerFnType == null || ownerFnType.isOrdinaryFunction() ?
448 |           null : ownerFnType.getInstanceType();
449 |       if (instType != null) {
450 |         thisType = instType;
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     return this;
455 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1365-1519
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1365 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1366 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1367 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1368 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1369 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1370 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1371 | 
1372 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1373 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1374 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1375 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1376 | 
1377 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1378 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1379 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1380 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1381 |         }
1382 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1383 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1384 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1385 | 
1386 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1387 |         return createOptionalType(
1388 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1389 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1390 | 
1391 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1392 |         return createOptionalType(
1393 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1394 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1395 | 
1396 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1397 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1398 | 
1399 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1400 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1401 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1402 | 
1403 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1404 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1405 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1406 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1407 |           builder.addAlternate(
1408 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1409 |         }
1410 |         return builder.build();
1411 | 
1412 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1413 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1414 | 
1415 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1416 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1417 | 
1418 |       case Token.STRING:
1419 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1420 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1421 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1422 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1423 |         }
1424 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1425 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1426 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1427 |           if (typeList != null &&
1428 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1429 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1430 |             JSType parameterType =
1431 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1432 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1433 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1434 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1435 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1436 |               JSType indexType =
1437 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1438 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1439 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1440 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1441 |             }
1442 |           }
1443 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1444 |         } else {
1445 |           return namedType;
1446 |         }
1447 | 
1448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1449 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1450 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1451 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1452 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1453 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1454 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1455 |           thisType =
1456 |               ObjectType.cast(
1457 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1458 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1459 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1460 |           if (thisType == null) {
1461 |             reporter.warning(
1462 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1463 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1464 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1465 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1466 |                 sourceName,
1467 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), "", contextNode.getCharno());
1468 |           }
1469 | 
1470 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1471 |           current = current.getNext();
1472 |         }
1473 | 
1474 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1475 | 
1476 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1477 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1478 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1479 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1480 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1481 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1482 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1483 |               } else {
1484 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1485 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1486 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1487 |               }
1488 |             } else {
1489 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1490 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1491 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1492 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1493 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1494 |                   reporter.warning(
1495 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1496 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1497 |                 }
1498 |               } else {
1499 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1500 |               }
1501 |             }
1502 |           }
1503 |           current = current.getNext();
1504 |         }
1505 | 
1506 |         JSType returnType =
1507 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1508 | 
1509 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1510 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1511 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1512 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1513 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1514 |             .build();
1515 |     }
1516 | 
1517 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1518 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1519 |   }

===== M0719 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getTypesUnderEquality(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 735-754
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the subset of {@code this} and {@code that} types if equality
   * is observed. If a value {@code v1} of type {@code null} is equal to a value
   * {@code v2} of type {@code (undefined,number)}, we can infer that the
   * type of {@code v1} is {@code null} and the type of {@code v2} is
   * {@code undefined}.
   *
   * @return a pair containing the restricted type of {@code this} as the first
   *         component and the restricted type of {@code that} as the second
   *         element. The returned pair is never {@code null} even though its
   *         components may be {@code null}
   */
Method code:
735 |   public TypePair getTypesUnderEquality(JSType that) {
736 |     // unions types
737 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
738 |       TypePair p = that.getTypesUnderEquality(this);
739 |       return new TypePair(p.typeB, p.typeA);
740 |     }
741 | 
742 |     // other types
743 |     switch (this.testForEquality(that)) {
744 |       case FALSE:
745 |         return new TypePair(null, null);
746 | 
747 |       case TRUE:
748 |       case UNKNOWN:
749 |         return new TypePair(this, that);
750 |     }
751 | 
752 |     // switch case is exhaustive
753 |     throw new IllegalStateException();
754 |   }

===== M0723 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 595-625
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
595 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
596 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
597 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
598 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
599 |     if (isOptional) {
600 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
601 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
602 |       // error can be reported.
603 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
604 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
605 |         emittedWarning = true;
606 |       }
607 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
608 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
609 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
610 |         emittedWarning = true;
611 |       }
612 |     } else {
613 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
614 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
615 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
616 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
617 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
618 |         } else {
619 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
620 |         }
621 |         emittedWarning = true;
622 |       }
623 |     }
624 |     return emittedWarning;
625 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 180-223
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
180 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
181 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
182 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
183 |     // show up in the type registry.
184 |     Scope newScope = null;
185 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
186 |     if (parent == null) {
187 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
188 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
189 | 
190 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
191 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
192 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
193 |     } else {
194 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
195 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
196 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
197 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
198 |     }
199 | 
200 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
201 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
202 | 
203 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
204 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
205 |     // build properties on.
206 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
207 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
208 |       if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
209 |         FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
210 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
211 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
212 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
213 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
214 |         }
215 |       }
216 |     }
217 | 
218 |     if (parent == null) {
219 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
220 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes);
221 |     }
222 |     return newScope;
223 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

===== M0763 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 413-441
Documentation:

Method code:
413 |     @Override
414 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n,
415 |         Node parent) {
416 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
417 |           n.getType() == Token.SCRIPT) {
418 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
419 |       }
420 | 
421 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
422 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
423 |       boolean descend = parent == null || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
424 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
425 | 
426 |       if (descend) {
427 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
428 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
429 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
430 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
431 |                child != null;
432 |                child = child.getNext()) {
433 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
434 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child, n);
435 |             }
436 |           }
437 |         }
438 |       }
439 | 
440 |       return descend;
441 |     }

/**
   * Declares a property on its owner, if necessary.
   * @return True if a property was declared.

611 |   private boolean ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(
612 |       Node getprop, ObjectType objectType) {
613 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
614 |     String qName = getprop.getQualifiedName();

624 |           return objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(

===== M0774 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.testForEqualityHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 511-549
Documentation:

Method code:
511 |   TernaryValue testForEqualityHelper(JSType aType, JSType bType) {
512 |     if (bType.isAllType() || bType.isUnknownType() ||
513 |         bType.isNoResolvedType() ||
514 |         aType.isAllType() || aType.isUnknownType() ||
515 |         aType.isNoResolvedType()) {
516 |       return UNKNOWN;
517 |     }
518 | 
519 |     boolean aIsEmpty = aType.isEmptyType();
520 |     boolean bIsEmpty = bType.isEmptyType();
521 |     if (aIsEmpty || bIsEmpty) {
522 |       if (aIsEmpty && bIsEmpty) {
523 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
524 |       } else {
525 |         return UNKNOWN;
526 |       }
527 |     }
528 | 
529 |     if (aType.isFunctionType() || bType.isFunctionType()) {
530 |       JSType otherType = aType.isFunctionType() ? bType : aType;
531 |       // In theory, functions are comparable to anything except
532 |       // null/undefined. For example, on FF3:
533 |       // function() {} == 'function () {\n}'
534 |       // In practice, how a function serializes to a string is
535 |       // implementation-dependent, so it does not really make sense to test
536 |       // for equality with a string.
537 |       JSType meet = otherType.getGreatestSubtype(
538 |           getNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE));
539 |       if (meet.isNoType() || meet.isNoObjectType()) {
540 |         return TernaryValue.FALSE;
541 |       } else {
542 |         return TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
543 |       }
544 |     }
545 |     if (bType.isEnumElementType() || bType.isUnionType()) {
546 |       return bType.testForEquality(aType);
547 |     }
548 |     return null;
549 |   }

171 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);

174 |     }

===== M0779 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 82-178
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
84 | 
85 |     switch (n.getType()) {
86 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
87 |       case Token.NAME:
88 |         if (parent == null) {
89 |           return;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
93 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
94 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
95 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
96 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
97 |           return;
98 |         }
99 | 
100 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
101 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
102 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
103 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
104 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
105 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
106 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
107 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
108 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
109 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
110 |         if (docInfo == null &&
111 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
113 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
114 |         }
115 | 
116 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
117 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
118 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
119 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
123 |         // we can do.
124 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
125 |           return;
126 |         }
127 | 
128 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
129 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
130 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
131 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 | 
135 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.GETPROP:
139 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
140 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
141 |         //
142 |         // 1)
143 |         // /** @deprecated */
144 |         // obj.prop = ...
145 |         //
146 |         // 2)
147 |         // /** @deprecated */
148 |         // obj.prop;
149 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
150 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
151 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
152 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
153 |           if (docInfo == null) {
154 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
155 |           }
156 |           if (docInfo != null) {
157 |             ObjectType lhsType =
158 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
159 |             if (lhsType != null) {
160 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
161 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
162 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
163 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
164 |               }
165 | 
166 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
167 |               ObjectType propType =
168 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
169 |               if (propType != null) {
170 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
171 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
172 |               }
173 |             }
174 |           }
175 |         }
176 |         break;
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0793 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1640-1679
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1640 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1641 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1642 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1643 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1644 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1645 |       // {?} - right curly
1646 |       // {?=} - equals
1647 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1648 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1649 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1650 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1651 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1652 |       token = next();
1653 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1654 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1655 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1656 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1657 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1658 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1659 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1660 |       }
1661 | 
1662 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1663 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1664 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1665 |     } else {
1666 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1667 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1668 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1669 |           next();
1670 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1671 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1672 |           next();
1673 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1674 |         }
1675 |       }
1676 | 
1677 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1678 |     }
1679 |   }

===== M0805 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1323-1431
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1323 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1324 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1325 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1326 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1327 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1328 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1329 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1330 | 
1331 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1332 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1333 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1334 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1335 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1336 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1337 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1338 |         if (qVar != null) {
1339 |           if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1340 |             // Just ignore assigns to declared prototypes.
1341 |             return;
1342 |           }
1343 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1344 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1345 |           }
1346 |         }
1347 |       }
1348 | 
1349 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1350 |       // 1) @type annnotation / @enum annotation
1351 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1352 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1353 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1354 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1355 |       //
1356 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1357 |       // the function has not been declared before.
1358 |       //
1359 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1360 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1361 | 
1362 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1363 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1364 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1365 |         // Determining type for #5
1366 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1367 |       }
1368 | 
1369 |       if (valueType == null) {
1370 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1371 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1372 |               n,
1373 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1374 |               ownerName));
1375 |         }
1376 | 
1377 |         return;
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       boolean inferred = true;
1381 |       if (info != null) {
1382 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3 + #4
1383 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1384 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1385 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1386 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1387 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1388 |       }
1389 | 
1390 |       if (inferred) {
1391 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1392 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1393 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1394 |             !scope.isDeclared(qName, false));
1395 |       }
1396 | 
1397 |       if (!inferred) {
1398 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1399 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1400 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1401 |           // declared yet.
1402 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1403 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1404 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1405 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1406 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1407 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1408 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, isExtern, n);
1409 |           }
1410 |         }
1411 | 
1412 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1413 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1414 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1415 |       } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1416 |           rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {
1417 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1418 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1419 |         if (ownerType instanceof FunctionType) {
1420 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ((FunctionType) ownerType).getTypeOfThis();
1421 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1422 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1423 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1424 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1425 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1426 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE),
1427 |                 true);
1428 |           }
1429 |         }

===== M0826 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 146-169
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
146 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
147 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
148 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
149 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
150 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
151 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
152 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
153 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
154 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
155 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
156 |           return false;
157 |         }
158 |       } else {
159 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
160 |           return false;
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
164 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
165 |     }
166 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
167 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
168 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
169 |   }

===== M0827 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 380-393
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
380 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
381 |       Node ret;
382 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
383 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
384 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
385 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
386 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
387 |       } else {
388 |         ret = transform(n);
389 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
390 |       }
391 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
392 |       return ret;
393 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken)

Documentation:
/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1092 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
1093 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1094 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1095 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
1096 | 
1097 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1098 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1099 | 
1100 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
1101 |         true);
1102 |     return typeNode;
1103 |   }

===== M0852 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

===== M0854 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 841-946
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.getType() == Token.ASSIGN</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
841 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
842 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
843 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
844 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
845 | 
846 |     if (lvalue.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
847 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
848 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
849 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
850 | 
851 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
852 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
853 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
854 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
855 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
856 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
857 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
858 |         }
859 |       }
860 | 
861 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
862 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
863 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
864 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
865 |             property, rvalue);
866 |         return;
867 |       }
868 | 
869 |       // /** @enum ... */object.name = ...;
870 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
871 |         checkEnumInitializer(
872 |             t, rvalue, info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(
873 |                 t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
874 |         return;
875 |       }
876 | 
877 |       // object.prototype = ...;
878 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
879 |         if (objectJsType instanceof FunctionType) {
880 |           FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) objectJsType;
881 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
882 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
883 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
884 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
885 |           }
886 |         } else {
887 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
888 |         }
889 |         return;
890 |       }
891 | 
892 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
893 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
894 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
895 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
896 | 
897 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
898 |           JSType jsType = object2.getJSType();
899 |           if (jsType instanceof FunctionType) {
900 |             FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jsType;
901 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
902 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
903 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
904 |             }
905 |           } else {
906 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
907 |           }
908 |           return;
909 |         }
910 |       }
911 | 
912 |       // object.property = ...;
913 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
914 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
915 |       if (type != null) {
916 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
917 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
918 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
919 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
920 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
921 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
922 |         }
923 |         return;
924 |       }
925 |     } else if (lvalue.getType() == Token.NAME) {
926 |       // variable with inferred type case
927 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
928 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
929 |       if (var != null) {
930 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
931 |           return;
932 |         }
933 |       }
934 |     }
935 | 
936 |     // fall through case
937 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
938 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
939 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
940 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
941 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
942 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
943 |     } else {
944 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
945 |     }
946 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2287-2303
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2287 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2288 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2289 |     int c;
2290 |     while (true) {
2291 |       c = stream.getChar();
2292 |       if (c == ' ') {
2293 |         continue;
2294 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2295 |         matchedLc = true;
2296 |         break;
2297 |       } else {
2298 |         break;
2299 |       }
2300 |     }
2301 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2302 |     return matchedLc;
2303 |   }

     * variable's type of {@link JSTypeNative#UNKNOWN_TYPE} if its type is

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java

/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
474 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
475 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
476 |     if (argsParent == null) {
477 |       if (info == null) {
478 |         return this;
479 |       } else {
480 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
481 |       }
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     // arguments
485 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
486 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
487 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
488 |     }
489 | 
490 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
491 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
492 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
493 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
494 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
495 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
496 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
497 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
498 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
499 | 
500 |       // type from JSDocInfo
501 |       JSType parameterType = null;
502 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
503 |       boolean isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
504 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
505 |         parameterType =
506 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
507 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
508 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
509 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
510 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
511 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
512 |       } else {
513 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
514 |       }
515 | 
516 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
517 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
518 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
519 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
520 |         }
521 |         foundTemplateType = true;
522 |       }
523 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
524 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
525 |           isOptionalParam,
526 |           isVarArgs);
527 | 
528 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
529 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
530 |       }
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
534 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
535 |     }
536 | 
537 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
538 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
539 |     }
540 | 
541 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
542 |     return this;
543 |   }

===== M0895 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 582-639
Documentation:

Method code:
582 |     @Override
583 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
584 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
585 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
586 |       if (name == null) {
587 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
588 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
589 |           errorReporter.error(
590 |             "unnamed function statement",
591 |             sourceName,
592 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
593 |         }
594 |         name = new Name();
595 |         name.setIdentifier("");
596 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
597 |       }
598 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
599 |       Node newName = transform(name);
600 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
601 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
602 |         // declaration.
603 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
604 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
605 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
606 |         // same line as the function keyword.
607 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
608 |             functionNode.getLp();
609 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
610 |       }
611 | 
612 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
613 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
614 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
615 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
616 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
617 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
618 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
619 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
620 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
621 |       if (fnName != null) {
622 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
623 |       } else {
624 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
625 |       }
626 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
627 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
628 | 
629 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
630 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
631 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
632 |       }
633 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
634 | 
635 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
636 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
637 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
638 |      return node;
639 |     }

===== M0905 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node,FlowScope,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 118-257
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   public FlowScope getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node condition,
119 |       FlowScope blindScope, boolean outcome) {
120 |     // Check for the typeof operator.
121 |     int operatorToken = condition.getType();
122 |     switch (operatorToken) {
123 |       case Token.EQ:
124 |       case Token.NE:
125 |       case Token.SHEQ:
126 |       case Token.SHNE:
127 |       case Token.CASE:
128 |         Node left;
129 |         Node right;
130 |         if (operatorToken == Token.CASE) {
131 |           left = condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
132 |           right = condition.getFirstChild();
133 |         } else {
134 |           left = condition.getFirstChild();
135 |           right = condition.getLastChild();
136 |         }
137 | 
138 |         Node typeOfNode = null;
139 |         Node stringNode = null;
140 |         if (left.getType() == Token.TYPEOF && right.getType() == Token.STRING) {
141 |           typeOfNode = left;
142 |           stringNode = right;
143 |         } else if (right.getType() == Token.TYPEOF &&
144 |                    left.getType() == Token.STRING) {
145 |           typeOfNode = right;
146 |           stringNode = left;
147 |         }
148 |         if (typeOfNode != null && stringNode != null) {
149 |           Node operandNode = typeOfNode.getFirstChild();
150 |           JSType operandType = getTypeIfRefinable(operandNode, blindScope);
151 |           if (operandType != null) {
152 |             boolean resultEqualsValue = operatorToken == Token.EQ ||
153 |                 operatorToken == Token.SHEQ || operatorToken == Token.CASE;
154 |             if (!outcome) {
155 |               resultEqualsValue = !resultEqualsValue;
156 |             }
157 |             return caseTypeOf(operandNode, operandType, stringNode.getString(),
158 |                 resultEqualsValue, blindScope);
159 |           }
160 |         }
161 |     }
162 |     switch (operatorToken) {
163 |       case Token.AND:
164 |         if (outcome) {
165 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
166 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
167 |         } else {
168 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
169 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
170 |         }
171 | 
172 |       case Token.OR:
173 |         if (!outcome) {
174 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
175 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
176 |         } else {
177 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
178 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
179 |         }
180 | 
181 |       case Token.EQ:
182 |         if (outcome) {
183 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
184 |         } else {
185 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
186 |         }
187 | 
188 |       case Token.NE:
189 |         if (outcome) {
190 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
191 |         } else {
192 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
193 |         }
194 | 
195 |       case Token.SHEQ:
196 |         if (outcome) {
197 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
198 |         } else {
199 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
200 |         }
201 | 
202 |       case Token.SHNE:
203 |         if (outcome) {
204 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
205 |         } else {
206 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
207 |         }
208 | 
209 |       case Token.NAME:
210 |       case Token.GETPROP:
211 |         return caseNameOrGetProp(condition, blindScope, outcome);
212 | 
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
214 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
215 |             condition.getFirstChild(),
216 |             firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
217 |                 condition.getFirstChild().getNext(), blindScope, outcome),
218 |             outcome);
219 | 
220 |       case Token.NOT:
221 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
222 |             condition.getFirstChild(), blindScope, !outcome);
223 | 
224 |       case Token.LE:
225 |       case Token.LT:
226 |       case Token.GE:
227 |       case Token.GT:
228 |         if (outcome) {
229 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, INEQ);
230 |         }
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
234 |         return caseInstanceOf(
235 |             condition.getFirstChild(), condition.getLastChild(), blindScope,
236 |             outcome);
237 | 
238 |       case Token.IN:
239 |         if (outcome && condition.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.STRING) {
240 |           return caseIn(condition.getLastChild(),
241 |               condition.getFirstChild().getString(), blindScope);
242 |         }
243 |         break;
244 | 
245 |       case Token.CASE:
246 |         Node left =
247 |             condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
248 |         Node right = condition.getFirstChild();
249 |         if (outcome) {
250 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHEQ);
251 |         } else {
252 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHNE);
253 |         }
254 |     }
255 |     return nextPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
256 |         condition, blindScope, outcome);
257 |   }

===== M0911 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1162-1170
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1162 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1163 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1164 |       return -1;
1165 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1166 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1167 |     } else {
1168 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1169 |     }
1170 |   }

===== M0913 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

/**
   * Handle cases #1 and #3 in the class doc.
   */
Method code:
190 |   private void attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
191 |       ObjectType objType, JSDocInfo docInfo, @Nullable String qName) {
192 |     if (objType.isConstructor() ||
193 |         objType.isEnumType() ||
194 |         objType.isInterface()) {
195 |       // Named types.
196 |       if (objType.hasReferenceName() &&
197 |           objType.getReferenceName().equals(qName)) {
198 |         objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
199 | 
200 |         if (objType.isConstructor() || objType.isInterface()) {
201 |           ((FunctionType) objType).getInstanceType().setJSDocInfo(
202 |               docInfo);
203 |         } else if (objType instanceof EnumType) {
204 |           ((EnumType) objType).getElementsType().setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
205 |         }
206 |       }
207 |     } else if (!objType.isNativeObjectType() &&
208 |         objType.isFunctionType()) {
209 |       // Structural functions.
210 |       objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
211 |     }
212 |   }

Method code:
991 |   private FlowScope traverseNew(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
992 |     Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
993 |     scope = traverse(constructor, scope);
994 | 
995 |     JSType constructorType = constructor.getJSType();
996 |     JSType type = null;
997 |     if (constructorType != null) {
998 |       constructorType = constructorType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
999 |       if (constructorType.isUnknownType()) {
1000 |         type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1001 |       } else if (constructorType instanceof FunctionType) {
1002 |         FunctionType ct = (FunctionType) constructorType;
1003 |         if (ct.isConstructor()) {
1004 |           type = ct.getInstanceType();
1005 |         }
1006 |       }
1007 |     }
1008 |     n.setJSType(type);
1009 | 
1010 |     for (Node arg = constructor.getNext(); arg != null; arg = arg.getNext()) {
1011 |       scope = traverse(arg, scope);
1012 |     }
1013 |     return scope;
1014 |   }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is inferred.
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param inExterns {@code true} if this property was defined in an externs
   *        file. TightenTypes assumes that any function passed to an externs
   *        property could be called, so setting this incorrectly could result
   *        in live code being removed.
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the inferred definition of
   *        property that might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.
   */
Method code:
272 |   public final boolean defineInferredProperty(String propertyName,
273 |       JSType type, boolean inExterns, Node propertyNode) {
274 |     if (hasProperty(propertyName)) {
275 |       JSType originalType = getPropertyType(propertyName);
276 |       type = originalType == null ? type :
277 |           originalType.getLeastSupertype(type);
278 |     }
279 | 
280 |     boolean result = defineProperty(propertyName, type, true, inExterns,
281 |         propertyNode);
282 | 
283 |     // All property definitions go through this method
284 |     // or defineDeclaredProperty. Because the properties defined an an
285 |     // object can affect subtyping, it's slightly more efficient
286 |     // to register this after defining the property.
287 |     registry.registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, this);
288 | 
289 |     return result;
290 |   }

Documentation:
/**
     * Find the function that's being overridden on this type, if any.
     */
Method code:
878 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
879 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName) {
880 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
881 |       // on a superclass.
882 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);
883 |       if (propType instanceof FunctionType) {
884 |         return (FunctionType) propType;
885 |       } else {
886 |         // If it's not, then check to see if it's implemented
887 |         // on an implemented interface.
888 |         for (ObjectType iface :
889 |                  ownerType.getCtorImplementedInterfaces()) {
890 |           propType = iface.getPropertyType(propName);
891 |           if (propType instanceof FunctionType) {
892 |             return (FunctionType) propType;
893 |           }
894 |         }
895 |       }
896 | 
897 |       return null;
898 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1591 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1592 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1593 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1594 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1595 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1596 |         next();
1597 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1598 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1599 |           next();
1600 |         }
1601 |         skipEOLs();
1602 |         token = next();
1603 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1604 |       }
1605 |     }
1606 |     return typeExpr;
1607 |   }

/**
   * Expect that all properties on interfaces that this type implements are
   * implemented and correctly typed.
   */
Method code:
568 |   void expectAllInterfaceProperties(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
569 |       FunctionType type) {
570 |     ObjectType instance = type.getInstanceType();
571 |     for (ObjectType implemented : type.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
572 |       if (implemented.getImplicitPrototype() != null) {
573 |         for (String prop :
574 |             implemented.getImplicitPrototype().getOwnPropertyNames()) {
575 |           expectInterfaceProperty(t, n, instance, implemented, prop);
576 |         }
577 |       }
